Across eight real-robot tasks spanning fine-grained, contact-rich, long-horizon, and bimanual manipulation, policies trained on RealDexUMI data achieve an average success rate of 88.75%, generalize to unseen initial poses, and transfer across three embodiments. Website: https://research.beingbeyond.com/realdexumi

</details>


### [72] [Gotta Grow Fast: Design and Benchmarking of a Tip Mount for High-Speed Vine Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06040)
*Antonio Alvarez Valdivia,Robert Reeve,Ankush Dhawan,Ciera McFarland,Chad Council,Margaret McGuinness,Nathaniel Hanson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三角滚轮尖端安装座，通过滚动代替滑动减少内部阻力，首次在TPU涂层防撕裂尼龙 vine 机器人上实现稳定外翻，并建立了量化评估测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: vine机器人在尖端安装摄像头和传感器面临挑战，因为尖端材料不断更新、内部摩擦、额外重量和织物收缩等因素导致传感器和工具安装困难，限制了其在检测和搜索任务中的应用。

Method: 设计三角滚轮尖端安装座，通过滚动替代滑动减少生长过程中的内部阻力；通过迭代故障分析优化设计；构建隔离尖端安装效果的定制测试平台，测量外翻过程中的尾部张力来量化评估安装座性能。

Result: 三角滚轮安装座相比先前设计实现了最低的尾部张力和最可重复的生长性能，首次在TPU涂层防撕裂尼龙vine机器人上实现一致外翻。

Conclusion: 建立了经过验证的尖端安装座设计和可重复的基准测试框架，推动了软体生长机器人中传感器和工具集成的进步。

Abstract: Soft, growing vine robots extend through tip eversion, a mechanism that enables navigation through cluttered environments. However, integrating cameras and other sensors at the tip is uniquely challenging because the material forming the tip is constantly renewed as the robot grows. This continual material turnover, combined with friction between internal layers, added tip weight, and fabric constriction, complicates sensor and tool mounting. These limitations hinder the deployment of vine robots for inspection and search tasks, where rapid growth while carrying tip-mounted sensors is essential. In this work, we present a triangular roller tip mount that reduces internal resistance during growth by rolling rather than sliding against the robot body. The design was refined through iterative failure analysis, enabling, for the first time, consistent eversion on a TPU-coated ripstop nylon vine robot. To quantitatively evaluate mount performance, we introduce a custom testbed that isolates tip mounting effects by measuring tail tension during eversion. Comparative experiments across multiple mount variants, including prior designs, show that our triangular roller mount achieves the lowest tail tension and most repeatable growth performance. These results establish both a validated tip mount design and a repeatable benchmarking framework for advancing sensor and tool integration in soft growing robots. CAD for the mount and testbed is available at: https://sprout-mitll.github.io/tip_mounts/.

</details>


### [73] [Sample-efficient Low-level Motion Planning for Robotic Manipulation Tasks via Zero-shot Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06041)
*Yuanzhi He,Victor Romero-Cano,José J. Patiño,Juan David Hernández,William Sawtell,Gualtiero Colombo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出iCEM+TL框架，通过迁移学习将简单任务中的iCEM参数迁移到复杂任务，并采用奖励重塑进行任务分解，在仿真和真实机器人上分别实现了23%的成功率提升和实际部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人运动规划模型日益复杂、训练时间增长，iCEM在复杂场景（如堆叠、滑动、货架放置）中性能受限，需要更高效的知识复用策略。

Method: 提出iCEM+TL框架，利用迁移学习将上游简单任务的iCEM关键参数迁移到下游复杂任务，并通过奖励重塑（RR）对堆叠和货架放置任务进行任务分解以优化性能。

Result: 仿真中成功率提升最高达23%；在真实Franka Emika机器人堆叠任务中验证了实际可行性。

Conclusion: iCEM+TL框架能有效提升复杂机器人运动规划任务的性能，并具备实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: As robotic systems become more sophisticated, the growing complexity of their motion planning models and the longer training times pose substantial challenges. Evolutionary algorithms such as the Sample-efficient Cross-Entropy Method (iCEM) have recently demonstrated promising potential for low-level real-time planning by leveraging efficient knowledge reuse strategies to improve performance. Although effective in many control tasks, iCEM's performance can be constrained in more complex scenarios, particularly those requiring stacking, sliding, and shelf placement. In this work, we propose a novel iCEM+TL framework that explicitly leverages Transfer Learning (TL), where key iCEM parameters are transferred from simpler upstream tasks to guide more complex downstream tasks. Additionally, we applied Reward Redesign (RR) through task decomposition for stacking objects and shelf placement to optimize task-specific performance. Results from the simulation show that our framework achieves success rate improvements of up to 23%. The framework is further validated on a real Franka Emika robot in a stacking task, demonstrating its practical feasibility for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [74] [L-SDPPO: Policy Optimization of Spiking Diffusion Policy for Intra-vehicular Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06049)
*Liwen Zhang,Dong Zhou,Guanghui Sun,Yifei Zheng,Yuhui Hu,Kaihong Ouyang,Zuoquan Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向航天器舱内机器人的低能耗操作框架L-SDPPO，结合脉冲扩散策略（SDP）与强化学习（RL），并引入状态依赖延迟注入（SDLI）机制模拟生物神经延迟以增强微重力下动态时空特征感知，在五项典型舱内任务中实现了更高成功率和更低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 舱内机器人有助于减少宇航员工作负担并提升任务效率，现有深度学习方法虽能实现复杂环境下的精细控制，但微重力下物体存在不可预测的非约束漂移，且航天器电力预算有限，扩散策略迭代采样过程能耗过高，亟需低能耗且鲁棒的操控方案。

Method: 提出L-SDPPO框架：1) 将脉冲扩散策略（SDP）与强化学习（RL）联合优化以降低能耗；2) 设计状态依赖延迟注入（SDLI）机制，通过模拟生物神经延迟动态调控输入信息时序，增强微重力下动态时空特征感知能力。

Result: 在开舱门、精密容器盖帽等五项代表性舱内日常任务上，L-SDPPO相比现有最优机器人操控方法取得了更高的成功率，同时能耗更低。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种可行的舱内机器人操控方案，兼顾了高成功率和低能耗，适用于航天器电力受限场景。

Abstract: Intra-vehicular robots in spacecraft help reduce astronaut workload and improve mission efficiency. Recent research focuses on using deep learning methods to achieve the acute control required for operations in these complex environments. However, objects exhibit unpredictable, unconstrained drift without gravitational damping. These factors demand robustness against complex multimodal action distributions. Diffusion policies (DP) can model these complex actions, but their iterative sampling process consumes too much energy for the limited power budgets of spacecraft. We therefore propose a low-energy intra-vehicular robotic manipulation framework, L-SDPPO, in which the Spiking Diffusion Policy (SDP) is optimized with a reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm. Furthermore, to address the insufficient perception of dynamic spatiotemporal features in microgravity, we propose the statedependent latency injection (SDLI) mechanism, which mimics biological neural delays to dynamically regulate the timing of input information. Evaluation on five representative intra-vehicular daily tasks (e.g., hatch opening and precision container capping) shows that our method consistently achieves higher success rates and lower energy consumption, compared to the state-of-the-art robotic manipulation methods. These results demonstrate our method is a viable intra-vehicular robotic manipulation method.

</details>


### [75] [A Conversational Framework for Human-Robot Collaborative Manipulation with Distributed Generative AI models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06061)
*Arash Ghasemzadeh Kakroudi,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于ROS 2的分布式人机协作操作对话框架，集成语言和视觉-语言模型，实现从自由形式用户指令到抓取、放置、移交等结构化动作的完整流程，并在Franka FR3平台上验证了端到端任务可靠性与延迟性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机协作操作系统中，语言理解和视觉定位往往紧密耦合或集中处理，缺乏灵活分布部署的能力，且对用户自由形式指令的结构化解析和可解释性反馈不足。本文旨在构建一个模块化、分布式、可解释的人机协作框架，提升系统的灵活性和安全性。

Method: 提出分布式对话框架，各模块（语言理解、视觉定位、任务编排、运动执行）作为独立ROS 2节点运行；使用VLM从自由形式指令生成图像空间目标点，结合深度图与标定参数转换为机器人坐标系目标；设计Web仪表盘展示中间意图和定位覆盖层（像素、深度、机器人坐标系），并要求操作员显式确认后方可执行运动。

Result: 在Franka FR3平台上进行了端到端任务可靠性及延迟实验，在逐步增加的工作台场景模糊度条件下评估系统性能，并比较了不同LLM/VLM配置在同一管道中的表现。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了语言理解、视觉定位与运动执行的分布式解耦，通过显式确认机制增强了系统安全性，在复杂场景下保持了较好的任务可靠性，展示了模块化集成LLM/VLM在人机协作操作中的实用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a distributed conversational framework for human-robot collaborative manipulation that integrates local language and vision-language models (VLMs) with a Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2)-based execution stack. Language understanding, visual grounding, orchestration, and motion execution run as separate ROS 2 nodes, enabling flexible deployment across distributed hardware while maintaining a responsive control loop. From free-form user commands, the system generates structured action requests for pick, place, and handover. It uses a VLM to return image-space targets, which are converted into metric robot-frame goals using depth and calibration. A web dashboard exposes intermediate intent and grounding overlays (pixel, depth, and robot-frame) and requires explicit operator confirmation before any motion is executed. Experiments on a Franka FR3 platform evaluate end-to-end task reliability and latency under increasing working table scene ambiguity and compare alternative LLM/VLM configurations in the same pipeline. Code and full documentation are available at [github.com/cogrob-tuni/franka-llm](https://github.com/cogrob-tuni/franka-llm).

</details>


### [76] [3D Underwater Path Planning via Generative Flow Field Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06077)
*Zachary Cooper-Baldock,Paulo E. Santos,Russell S. A. Brinkworth,Karl Sammut*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用条件生成对抗网络（cGAN）作为计算流体动力学（CFD）的替代模型，用于自主水下航行器（AUV）在复杂螺旋桨尾流中的路径规划。两种cGAN架构（PatchGAN和2D3DGAN-SA）能以微秒级推理时间生成128³体素流场，替代耗时数小时的RANS CFD计算。在19,800条轨迹上的基准测试表明，CFD知识可降低能耗5.7-12.5%并减少高速尾流核心遭遇达77.8%，cGAN替代模型能恢复约45-60%的CFD收益，且推理速度适合边缘设备部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV在母船船体中进行布放与回收（LAR）时需穿越复杂的三维螺旋桨尾流，其水动力学结构无法用均匀流模型表征。高保真RANS CFD仿真能精确解析该结构但计算成本极高，无法用于机载实时路径规划，因此需要一种高效且准确的替代方案。

Method: 集成两种cGAN架构（正则化PatchGAN和带自注意力的2D3DGAN）作为RANS CFD数据的替代模型；设计分层流水线从标量操作条件输入合成完整的128³体素流场；在三维能量加权A*路径规划框架中，对四种环境知识水平（均匀流、真实CFD、PatchGAN、2D3DGAN-SA）进行系统基准测试。

Result: CFD全知识相较均匀流规划降低能耗5.7-12.5%，高速尾流核心遭遇减少高达77.8%；cGAN替代模型恢复约45-60%的CFD节能效益和高速单元规避效益；cGAN推理时间为28-146微秒，而单次RANS计算需数小时。

Conclusion: cGAN预测的水动力学场在三维海洋机器人路径规划中具有可量化的下游价值，能够在接近CFD性能的同时实现适合边缘设备的超快推理速度，为实际AUV布放回收操作中的实时路径规划提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) launch and recovery (LAR) into the hull of an advancing host platform requires traversal of a complex, three-dimensional propeller wake whose hydrodynamic structure cannot be characterised by a uniform current model. High-fidelity Reynolds-Averaged Navier-Stokes (RANS) Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations resolve this structure with sufficient accuracy for path planning, but their computational cost renders them impractical for onboard use. We address this gap by integrating two conditional generative adversarial network (cGAN) architectures -- a regularised PatchGAN and a 2D3DGAN with self-attention -- as drop-in replacements for RANS CFD data within a three-dimensional, energy-weighted A* path planning framework. Both generators are driven by a hierarchical pipeline that synthesises full $128^3$ voxel flow field volumes from scalar operating condition inputs alone, with end-to-end inference times of approximately 28-146 $μ$s, compared to hours for a single RANS computation. We benchmark all four environmental knowledge levels: uniform current, ground-truth CFD, PatchGAN, and 2D3DGAN~SA across 19,800 independently generated trajectories spanning 550 distinct flow conditions. Full CFD wake knowledge reduces energy expenditure by 5.7-12.5% and high-velocity wake-core encounters by up to 77.8% relative to uniform-current planning, with both benefits scaling with operating severity. The cGAN surrogates recover approximately 45-60% of the CFD energy benefit and high-velocity cell avoidance benefit while operating at inference speeds compatible with edge device use. These results provide the first systematic quantification of the downstream path planning value of cGAN-predicted hydrodynamic fields in a three-dimensional maritime robotics application.

</details>


### [77] [Towards Realistic 3D Sonar Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06130)
*Youssef Attia,Davide Costa,Francesco Wanderlingh,Filippo Campagnaro,Enrico Simetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化架构的3D声纳仿真系统，在NVIDIA Isaac Sim环境中集成GPU加速图形引擎与物理声学传播原理，通过硬件在环配置验证，并与真实港口钢板桩检测数据进行了定性对比，揭示了仿真到现实的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有声纳仿真框架通常采用几何驱动渲染，将3D声纳近似为水下等效LiDAR，无法模拟折射、多径干扰和相位相关信号形成等基础声学现象，严重制约了复杂3D感知与自主导航算法的发展。

Method: 提出模块化架构，将GPU加速图形引擎与物理声学传播原理相结合；在NVIDIA Isaac Sim中实现基于Water Linked 3D-15传感器的体素化3D声纳模型；通过硬件在环配置（NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano运行FastLIO2 SLAM）进行传感器融合验证。

Result: 通过修改后的FastLIO2 SLAM管线成功融合了合成3D声纳、DVL、IMU和压力数据；提供了模拟输出与真实港口钢板桩检测数据的定性对比，表征了仿真到现实之间的差距。

Conclusion: 当前系统仍存在仿真到现实的差距，未来需要向完全声学驱动的体素化感知方向发展。

Abstract: As underwater robotics research increasingly addresses complex 3D perception and autonomous navigation, the fidelity of sonar simulation has become a key factor in algorithm development. Current simulation frameworks typically rely on geometry-driven rendering, approximating 3D sonar as an underwater equivalent to LiDAR, which fails to account for fundamental acoustic phenomena such as refraction, multi-path interference, and phase-dependent signal formation. This paper proposes a modular architecture for realistic 3D sonar simulation that integrates GPU-accelerated graphics engines with physically grounded acoustic propagation principles. We implement a volumetric 3D sonar model within the NVIDIA Isaac Sim environment, modeled after the Water Linked 3D-15 sensor, and integrate it into a comprehensive underwater simulation framework. The system is validated through a hardware-in-the-loop configuration, where a modified FastLIO2 SLAM pipeline, executed on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano, performs sensor fusion using synthetic 3D sonar, DVL, IMU, and pressure data. Finally, a qualitative comparison between simulated outputs and real-world data from harbor sheet-pile inspections is provided, characterizing the remaining sim-to-real gap and establishing a roadmap toward fully acoustics-driven volumetric sensing.

</details>


### [78] [MotionDisco: Motion Discovery for Extreme Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06139)
*Ilyass Taouil,Michal Ciebelski,Shafeef Omar,Haizhou Zhao,Angela Dai,Aaron M. Johnson,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionDisco 是一个无需遥操作或人类演示即可从零发现类人机器人接触式长时域移动操作动作的框架，通过LLM引导的进化搜索与运动动力学轨迹优化相结合，首次实现了完全自动化进化搜索下的长时域类人移动操作技能发现与真实机器人部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从零发现类人机器人接触丰富、长时域的移动操作动作极具挑战性，因为接触交互空间随任务时域和场景物体数量呈组合爆炸式增长，且现有方法依赖遥操作或人类演示重定向，缺乏自动发现能力。

Method: 将大语言模型引导的进化搜索与高效序列运动动力学轨迹优化器及剪枝策略耦合，在交互序列空间中进行搜索，并对发现轨迹训练强化学习跟踪策略以迁移到真实机器人。

Result: 在多个挑战性长时域任务上发现了成功的全身轨迹；通过训练RL跟踪策略成功将动作迁移到真实类人机器人。

Conclusion: 这是首个完全通过自动化进化搜索发现并部署长时域类人移动操作技能的工作，证明了LLM引导搜索在复杂接触式任务中的有效性。

Abstract: We present MotionDisco, a framework that discovers contact-rich, long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation motions from scratch, without relying on teleoperation or motion retargeting from human demonstrations. This is challenging because the space of possible contact interactions grows combinatorially with the task horizon and the number of objects in the scene. MotionDisco enables rapid discovery of novel motions by coupling a large language model (LLM) guided evolutionary search over sequences of interactions with an efficient sequential kinodynamic trajectory optimizer and pruning strategy, enabling the rapid discovery of novel skills. Through extensive ablation studies, we show that our LLM-guided search discovers successful whole-body trajectories across several challenging long-horizon tasks. Finally, by training reinforcement learning tracking policies on the discovered trajectories, we transfer the motions to a real humanoid robot. This is the first work to discover and deploy long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation skills entirely through automated evolutionary search. Supplementary videos of the experiments are available at: https://youtu.be/DHiVz34QYlw.

</details>


### [79] [AffordanceVLA: A Vision-Language-Action Model Empowering Action Generation through Affordance-Aware Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06155)
*Qize Yu,Jiadi You,Yuran Wang,Jiaqi Liang,Bowen Ping,Yang Tian,Yue Chen,Minghong Cai,Zeying Gong,Ruihai Wu,Yinchuan Li,Junwei Liang,Yingcong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AffordanceVLA通过将结构化的 affordance 预测作为任务导向的中间表示，建立更精确的感知-动作映射，采用Which2Act、Where2Act、How2Act三阶段模块和混合Transformer架构，在模拟和真实场景的机器人操作任务中取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型利用预训练VLM的丰富世界知识进行指令跟随机器人操作，但VLM语义空间与具身控制策略之间存在结构不匹配，阻碍了精确感知-动作映射的学习。

Method: 提出AffordanceVLA框架：1) Which2Act通过视觉潜在预测抑制干扰；2) Where2Act通过affordance图估计进行2D交互定位；3) How2Act进行3D几何推理引导操作策略。采用MoT架构和三阶段训练策略，并开发自动化数据增强流水线。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界实验中，AffordanceVLA在多种操作场景中均展现出强劲性能。

Conclusion: 结构化的affordance预测作为中间表示能有效弥合VLM语义空间与具身控制策略之间的鸿沟，提升VLA模型的感知-动作映射精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models leverage the rich world knowledge of pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) to enable instruction-following robotic manipulation. However, the structural mismatch between VLM semantic spaces and embodied control policies often hinders the learning of precise perception--action mappings. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{AffordanceVLA}, a unified framework that introduces structured affordance forecasting as a task-oriented intermediate representation to establish a more precise and robust perception--action mapping. Specifically, we progressively model manipulation priors through three complementary components: 1) \textbf{Which2Act} for object-centric grounding via visual latent prediction to suppress distractions; 2) \textbf{Where2Act} for 2D interaction localization via affordance map estimation; and 3) \textbf{How2Act} for 3D geometric reasoning to guide manipulation policies. These affordance cues provide spatially grounded, semantically conditioned, and action-coupled intermediate representations, thereby naturally bridging vision, language and action. We integrate these modules into a Mixture-of-Transformer (MoT) architecture with specialized experts and train the model using a three-stage training strategy with a progressive data curriculum. To overcome the scarcity of dense affordance labels in robotic datasets, we also develop a robust automated data augmentation pipeline. Extensive experiments on simulation and real-world demonstrate that AffordanceVLA achieves strong performance across diverse manipulation scenarios.

</details>


### [80] [ActiveMimic: Egocentric Video Pretraining with Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06194)
*Xingyao Lin,Guojin Zhong,Tianyi Lu,Ziyi Ye,Yichen Zhu,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActiveMimic框架通过从第一人称人类视频中恢复摄像机与手腕轨迹，将主动感知行为（摄像机运动）建模为视角动作，实现与操作行为的联合学习，从而弥合人类视频与机器人数据预训练之间的性能差距，首次使纯人类视频预训练达到与机器人数据预训练相当的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以自我为中心的人类视频在机器人预训练中效果远不如机器人数据，根本原因在于缺乏主动感知行为信号——人类在操作过程中不断调整视角产生的摄像机运动被标准流水线当作噪声丢弃。

Method: 从单台身体佩戴RGB摄像头中同步恢复摄像机轨迹和手腕轨迹；将摄像机运动显式建模为视角动作（viewpoint action）；在野外第一人称人类视频上联合学习主动感知与操作行为；再迁移适应到目标机器人。

Result: 在多项需要主动感知的真实世界任务中，ActiveMimic持续超越基于人类视频预训练的基线方法，并与基于机器人数据预训练的最先进模型性能持平。

Conclusion: 主动感知能力源自第一人称人类视频预训练而非机器人特定微调，证实主动感知是解锁第一人称人类视频用于机器人预训练的关键信号。

Abstract: Egocentric human video offers a scalable alternative to robot data for pretraining, yet models pretrained on such video consistently underperform those pretrained on robot data. We attribute this gap to a missing signal, the active perception behavior in egocentric videos, where humans continuously reposition their viewpoint during manipulation, inducing camera motion that standard pipelines treat as noise. To address this, we present ActiveMimic, a pretraining framework that recovers synchronized camera and wrist trajectories from a single body-worn RGB camera, models camera motion as a viewpoint action, and jointly learns active perception and manipulation from in-the-wild egocentric human video before adapting to a target robot. Empirically, real-world experiments across tasks with diverse active perception demands show that ActiveMimic consistently surpasses baselines pretrained on human video and matches state-of-the-art models pretrained on robot data. Further analysis provides evidence that active perception capability originates from egocentric human video pretraining rather than robot-specific fine-tuning, confirming active perception as the key to unlocking egocentric human video for robot pretraining.

</details>


### [81] [TAM: Torque Adaptation Module for Robust Motion Transfer in Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06218)
*Dongwon Son,Florian Shkurti,Jason Lee,Naman Shah,Beomjoon Kim,Dieter Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出扭矩适应模块(TAM)，通过学习修正扭矩指令来弥合不同机器人间的动力学差异，使策略能零样本迁移到真实机器人上执行动态操作任务，无需领域随机化或真实数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一策略在不同机器人实例上因动力学差异（如sim-to-real差距、未知负载等）表现迥异，尤其是在接触丰富的动态操作中，微小运动偏差会导致接触时序和模式紊乱，任务失败。现有方法如领域随机化使策略过于保守，系统辨识则需为每台机器人重新采集数据。

Method: TAM是一个学习模块，位于底层控制器与机器人扭矩接口之间。包含：1) 历史编码器：将本体感知历史嵌入隐状态；2) 扭矩适配器：计算残差扭矩修正。TAM仅依赖本体感知历史，不依赖策略观测或动作空间，因此同一组TAM权重可适配不同动作空间的策略。训练采用多机器人预训练+机器人特定微调，均在随机化仿真中完成，无需真实数据。

Result: 在真实Franka Panda机器人上零样本测试三种动态操作任务（基于RL的视觉推箱、基于BC的翻转、MPC的球平衡），TAM相比在线系统辨识和RMA基线显著提升了真实机器人执行效果，实现了鲁棒的动态操作性能。

Conclusion: TAM通过解耦动力学适应与策略学习，为跨机器人策略迁移提供了一种高效、无需真实数据和领域随机化的解决方案，在动态接触丰富的操作任务中表现出优越的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: A policy tuned for one robot often behaves differently on another, whether due to the sim-to-real gap, unknown payloads, or the differing dynamics of two instances of the same robot. In contact-rich, dynamic manipulation, even small motion discrepancies can result in failure to track reference motion, since they disrupt the timing and modes of contact. Common remedies, such as domain randomization or system identification, either produce overly conservative task policies or require data that must be recollected for each robot or payload. We introduce the Torque Adaptation Module (TAM), a learned module that adapts the torque commands sent to the robot to match the behavior of an ideal robot. TAM operates between the low-level controller that tracks the policy's actions and the robot's torque interface. It includes a history encoder that embeds proprioceptive history into a latent state and a torque adaptor that computes residual torque corrections. Because TAM depends only on proprioceptive history and not on policy observations, or the action space, the same TAM weights can be reused to adapt policies with different action spaces (joint targets, end-effector targets, or direct torques). The policies themselves do not need to be trained with domain randomization of robot parameters. Instead, we offload the need for domain randomization to TAM by training it entirely in randomized simulation, using multi-robot pretraining followed by a robot-specific fine-tuning step that still requires no real-robot data. We evaluate TAM zero-shot on a real Franka Panda robot across dynamic manipulation tasks that include a vision-based box pushing policy (from RL), a flip policy (from BC), and an MPC ball-on-plate balancing. Our experiments show that TAM improves zero-shot real-robot execution compared to online system identification and RMA baselines and enables robust dynamic manipulation performance.

</details>


### [82] [CLEAR: Cognition and Latent Evaluation for Adaptive Routing in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06219)
*Yining Xing,Zehong Ke,Zhiyuan Liu,Yanbo Jiang,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLEAR框架通过单步条件漂移替代扩散模型的多步去噪过程，结合Drive-JEPA视觉编码器和Qwen 3.5B语言模型，在NAVSIM v1基准上达到93.7 PDMS的SOTA性能，实现了高效的多模态轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型难以兼顾多模态轨迹生成与实时推理约束，扩散模型的迭代去噪过程引入无法容忍的延迟，无法满足安全关键场景的部署需求。

Method: 采用Drive-JEPA作为视觉编码器，用VAE潜空间中的单步条件漂移替代多步去噪链，引入调节系数α平衡多样性与专家精度；微调Qwen 3.5B模型提取场景感知隐状态，驱动自适应调度器（选择α和采样数N）和交叉注意力评分器（选择最优轨迹）。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1基准上取得93.7 PDMS的SOTA结果，证明高保真多模态规划可在无需密集几何标注或迭代采样的条件下高效执行。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保证轨迹多样性和精度的同时大幅降低推理延迟，为安全关键的自驾部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models often struggle to balance multi-modal maneuver generation with real-time inference constraints. While diffusion models successfully capture diverse driving behaviors, their iterative denoising process incurs unacceptable latency for safety-critical deployment. To address this, we propose CLEAR (Cognition and Latent Evaluation for Adaptive Routing), a framework that combines ultra-fast generative planning with deep semantic reasoning. CLEAR employs Drive-JEPA as the visual encoder and replaces the multi-step denoising chain with a single-step conditional drift in a VAE latent space, introducing a conditioning coefficient to balance diversity and expert precision. Meanwhile, we fully fine-tune Qwen~3.5~0.8B on driving QA pairs to extract scene-aware hidden states. These states guide both an Adaptive Scheduler, which selects the conditioning coefficient $α$ and sample count $N$ from a discrete set of predefined schemes, and a cross-attention scorer that selects the optimal trajectory from candidates. On the NAVSIM v1 benchmark, CLEAR achieves a state-of-the-art PDMS of 93.7. Our results demonstrate that high-fidelity, multi-modal planning can be executed efficiently without dense geometric annotations or iterative sampling.

</details>


### [83] [MPCoT: Reward-Guided Multi-Path Latent Reasoning for Test-Time Scalable Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06245)
*Boyang Zhang,Lianlei Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MPCoT是一种奖励引导的多路径潜在推理框架，通过多假设初始化、权值共享的K步迭代优化和软聚合机制提升VLA策略在长时域和高不确定性控制中的性能，无需生成显式推理token，保持原有8步动作接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA策略在长时域和高不确定性控制任务中表现脆弱，单次动作解码缺乏推理时深思熟虑能力；显式思维链虽能增加推理深度，但引入token延迟和间接文本到动作接口的问题。

Method: 提出MPCoT框架：初始化M个假设路径，经过K个权值共享的步骤进行迭代优化，然后通过软聚合（置信度加权）融合多路径信息后解码动作。训练阶段引入路径偏好目标函数，结合专家动作一致性、世界模型/VLM进度评估和成功反馈来对齐潜在路径评分器与下游执行质量。

Result: 在LIBERO和CALVIN基准测试的标准协议下，MPCoT显著提升了长时域任务性能；消融实验验证了深度-宽度效应、置信度加权聚合和奖励引导路径监督的有效性。

Conclusion: MPCoT在不增加推理token、保持原始动作接口的前提下，通过多路径潜在推理有效提升了VLA策略的性能，并提供了可配置的推理控制参数（K, M），为长时域机器人控制提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies remain brittle in long-horizon and high-uncertainty control, where one-pass action decoding provides limited inference-time deliberation. Explicit chain-of-thought can increase reasoning depth, but introduces token latency and an indirect text-to-action interface. We propose MPCoT, a reward-guided multi-path latent reasoning framework that initializes $M$ hypotheses, refines them for K weight-tied steps, and softly aggregates them before action decoding. A training-only path-preference objective evaluates candidate action branches with expert-action consistency, world-model/VLM-based progress, and success feedback to align the latent path scorer with downstream execution quality. MPCoT preserves the original 8-step action interface, generates zero reasoning tokens, and exposes configurable inference controls (K,M). Under matched protocols on LIBERO and CALVIN, MPCoT improves long-horizon performance, with ablations confirming depth-width effects, confidence-weighted aggregation, and reward-guided path supervision.

</details>


### [84] [Breaking Time: A Fully Gaussian Framework for Distributed and Continuous-Time SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06250)
*Davide Ceriola,Simone Ferrari,Luca Di Giammarino,Leonardo Brizi,Giorgio Grisetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出G-solver，一种结合高斯信念传播（GBP）与高斯过程（GP）运动先验的全高斯分布式连续时间SLAM框架。GP提供概率轨迹表示与数据驱动超参数，GBP实现可扩展的消息传递求解。该框架天然支持多相机场景，无需专门同步，在滚动快门与分布式多相机优化中取得与现有方法相当的精度与运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间SLAM方法在处理异构、异步传感器（如滚动快门相机、LiDAR、雷达、事件相机）时缺乏统一的分布式概率框架，难以兼顾轨迹平滑性、传感器融合的可扩展性以及多相机场景下的解耦同步需求。

Method: 将高斯过程（GP）作为轨迹的连续时间运动先验，提供概率化插值与数据驱动超参数学习；采用高斯信念传播（GBP）作为分布式消息传递求解器，实现去中心化的因子图推理。两者结合形成全高斯框架，天然支持异构异步传感器融合与多相机扩展。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上（包括滚动快门与分布式多相机优化）进行评估，表明该方法在估计精度和稳定性上与现有连续时间方法相当，且运行时间具有可比性。

Conclusion: G-solver提供了一种可扩展、分布式的连续时间SLAM求解方案，利用GP与GBP的概率一致性，简化了多传感器融合与多相机系统的工程实现，开源实现有利于社区进一步研究与应用。

Abstract: Continuous-time SLAM provides a principled framework for fusing heterogeneous sensors while estimating smooth trajectories, and is particularly well-suited for handling heterogeneous, asynchronous sensor streams with non-uniform readout patterns, such as rolling shutter cameras, LiDAR scanners, radar sweeps, or event-based sensors. In this work, we introduce G-solver, a fully Gaussian and distributed framework that combines Gaussian Belief Propagation (GBP) with Gaussian Process (GP) motion priors for continuous-time trajectory estimation. Our GP model provides a probabilistic representation of the trajectory, enabling consistent interpolation and the use of data-driven hyperparameters, while GBP offers a scalable message-passing formulation well-suited for decentralized settings. The resulting solver naturally extends to multi-camera scenarios without specialized synchronization or engineering effort. We evaluate the approach on synthetic and real data, including rolling shutter and distributed multi-camera optimization, demonstrating accurate and stable estimation with runtimes comparable to existing continuous-time methods. An open-source implementation is released.

</details>


### [85] [RadiusFPS: Efficient Farthest Point Sampling on CPUs and GPUs via Spherical Voxel Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06255)
*Ziyang Yu,Xiang Li,Qiong Chang,Jun Miyazaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RadiusFPS 是一种基于球形体素剪枝的 FPS 加速框架，通过保守几何边界和坐标级点跳过测试减少冗余距离计算，其 GPU 实现 RadiusFPS-G 融合核函数消除全局内存往返，在室内外基准上实现最高 2.5 倍加速，内存减半，且保持分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 FPS 的时间复杂度与每秒百万点级别的现代 3D 传感器速率不匹配，成为实时机器人系统中延迟瓶颈，且与机载有限计算预算冲突。

Method: 提出 RadiusFPS 框架：1) 用球形体素索引点云，推导保守几何边界剪枝每轮冗余距离计算；2) 坐标级点跳过测试消除剩余更新；3) GPU 实现 RadiusFPS-G 采用 warp 级融合核，将体素选择、剪枝和距离更新合并为内存合并核，消除全局内存往返。

Result: 在 S3DIS、ScanNet（室内）和 SemanticKITTI（室外 LiDAR）上，RadiusFPS-G 比 GPU FPS 加速最高 2.5 倍，与 QuickFPS 相当或更优，GPU 内存仅为其一半，分割精度可比；与 FastPoint 结合实现最快端到端推理。

Conclusion: RadiusFPS 使高质量 FPS 采样在延迟和内存受限的机器人视觉系统中变得实用。

Abstract: Point clouds are a primary sensory representation for robotic perception, underpinning LiDAR-based autonomous driving, simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM), and navigation. Within these pipelines, Farthest Point Sampling (FPS) is the most well-known downsampling operator, as its uniform coverage preserves the geometric structure on which downstream perception relies. However, the large time complexity of classical FPS scales poorly with the million-point-per-second rates of modern 3D sensors, making it a dominant latency bottleneck that conflicts with the real-time and limited onboard compute budgets of robotic systems. Therefore, we propose RadiusFPS, an FPS acceleration framework based on spherical voxel pruning that preserves the standard FPS update rule under the same initialization and tie-breaking policy. By indexing the point cloud with spherical voxels, RadiusFPS derives a conservative geometric bound that prunes redundant distance computations in each iteration, complemented by a coordinate-wise point-skip test that removes residual updates. We further introduce RadiusFPS-G, a warp-level GPU implementation that fuses voxel selection, pruning, and distance update into memory-coalesced kernels, eliminating costly global-memory round-trips. On indoor (S3DIS, ScanNet) and outdoor LiDAR (SemanticKITTI) benchmarks, RadiusFPS-G attains up to 2.5x speedup over GPU-based FPS and matches or exceeds QuickFPS among the evaluated methods while using roughly half its GPU memory, with comparable segmentation accuracy. When coupled with the learning-based FastPoint sampler, the resulting pipeline achieves the fastest End-to-End inference among all evaluated configurations. These properties make high-quality FPS-style sampling practical for latency- and memory-constrained robotic vision.

</details>


### [86] [Multi-Resolution Tactile Imitation Learning for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06281)
*Rickmer Krohn,Erik Helmut,Niklas Funk,Jan Peters,Vignesh Prasad,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MiTaS，一种多分辨率触觉感知框架，融合视觉、GelSight Mini（低频）和Evetac（高频事件触觉）传感器信息，基于模态特定卷积骨干和Transformer融合架构，通过流匹配策略实现复杂接触操控任务的模仿学习，在五项任务中达到80%成功率，远超视觉-only（31%）和视觉-触觉（54%）基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管存在多种不同性质的触觉传感器，利用异构触觉传感器的融合来提升操控学习的研究仍不足。现有方法通常仅使用单一触觉传感器，未能充分利用多分辨率触觉信息的互补优势。

Method: 提出MiTaS表示框架，核心架构包括：1）针对RGB相机、GelSight Mini（低频视触觉）和Evetac（高频事件触觉）分别使用模态特定卷积骨干提取特征；2）基于Transformer的融合机制整合多模态信息；3）融合后的多分辨率触觉表征作为条件，指导流匹配策略（flow-matching policy）生成动作。

Result: 在五项接触密集操控任务中，MiTaS平均成功率80%，视觉-only基线仅31%，视觉-触觉（单触觉）基线54%。此外，使用多触觉数据协同训练视觉-触觉模型可在某些任务中提升10%以上性能，且策略评估时无需Evetac传感器。

Conclusion: 多分辨率触觉感知融合能显著提升复杂接触操控任务的模仿学习性能；传感器读取和注意力分析验证了不同传感器在任务执行不同阶段的重要性，支持了多分辨率触觉方法的有效性。

Abstract: Touch sensing is beneficial for solving a wide variety of manipulation tasks. While there exists a wide range of tactile sensors with different properties, exploiting the fusion of multiple heterogeneous tactile sensors to improve manipulation learning remains underexplored. We present Multi-Resolution Tactile Sensing (MiTaS), a representation framework that leverages multiple tactile sensors operating at different temporal resolutions in order to solve complex contact-rich manipulation tasks. We propose a novel architecture using modality-specific convolutional stems and transformer-based fusion that effectively fuses information from an RGB camera stream, a vision-based GelSight Mini sensor and a high-frequency event-based Evetac sensor. This multi-sensor representation then conditions a flow-matching policy for solving downstream tasks. Experimental results across five contact-rich manipulation tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of multi-resolution tactile features in imitation learning. MiTaS achieves an average success rate of 80 %, while vision-only (31 %) and visual-tactile (54 %) baselines cannot solve the task reliably. Co-training a visuo-tactile model with multi-tactile data boosts performance by over 10 \% in certain tasks, without having access to the Evetac sensor during policy evaluation. A detailed sensor-reading and attention analysis reveals the importance of different sensors throughout task execution, validating our multi-resolution tactile sensing approach. Project Page: http://mitas-touch.github.io.

</details>


### [87] [Attitude-Aided Linear Calibration of Triaxial Accelerometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06308)
*Yongqiang Yu,Tian Huang,Yipeng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ALAC的加速度计线性标定方法，利用姿态信息构建组合误差矩阵，通过线性最小二乘实现标定，仅需5个任意方向测量即可在静态重力下完成，支持离线与在线模式，实验证明在精度和鲁棒性上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MEMS加速度计标定方法依赖昂贵参考设备或非线性迭代优化，效率低且难以适用于低成本或自标定系统，亟需一种高效、线性且无需专用参考设备的标定方案。

Method: 利用任意平台（转台、机械臂、IMU）提供的姿态信息，构建组合误差矩阵（CEM）统一表示传感器误差，通过线性最小二乘估计偏置和重力向量，并隐式处理平台对齐误差；进一步对CEM进行矩阵分解恢复尺度、非正交性及对齐旋转参数；静态重力下标定转化为约束齐次最小二乘问题，闭式求解，仅需5个任意方向测量；支持递归扩展以适应在线标定。

Result: 在机器人安装的加速度计和公共IMU轨迹数据集上，ALAC的离线和在线模式在精度和对噪声鲁棒性上均优于基于参考和在线标定的基线方法；在滤波条件下与迭代自标定持平，在原始测量上超越所有基线。

Conclusion: ALAC提供了一种鲁棒且实用的MEMS惯性平台标定方案，尤其适用于低成本IMU和在线标定场景。

Abstract: Triaxial MEMS accelerometers are widely used for inertial sensing, navigation, and sensor fusion, but existing calibration methods often rely on costly reference setups or nonlinear iterative optimization, limiting their efficiency and applicability to low-cost or self-calibrating systems. We present attitude-aided linear accelerometer calibration (ALAC), a method that operates on any platform providing orientation information, such as turntables, robotic arms, or inertial measurement units. ALAC constructs a combined error matrix (CEM) to represent sensor errors in a unified calibration model and enables linear least-squares estimation. The bias and gravity vector are jointly estimated, implicitly accounting for platform misalignment, and matrix decomposition of the CEM recovers scale, non-orthogonality, and alignment rotation parameters. Under static gravity, calibration is formulated as a constrained homogeneous least-squares (CHLS) problem and solved in closed form using standard linear algebra. Only five arbitrarily oriented measurements are required, and a recursive extension supports online or in-field calibration. Experiments on a stationary robot-mounted accelerometer and a quasi-static public IMU trajectory show that ALAC, in both offline and online modes, outperforms reference-based and online baselines in accuracy and robustness to sensor noise. On the same dataset, it matches iterative self-calibration under filtered conditions and surpasses all evaluated baselines on raw measurements. These results demonstrate a robust and practical calibration scheme for MEMS-based inertial platforms, especially low-cost IMUs and online calibration scenarios.

</details>


### [88] [Meridian: Metric-Semantic Primitive Matching for Cross-View Geo-Localization Beyond Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06312)
*Mason Peterson,Qingyuan Li,Yixuan Jia,Fernando Cladera,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Camillo Jose Taylor,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Meridian提出了一种无需区域特定训练或微调的方法，通过匹配航拍图像与地面机器人RGB-D数据中的高层度量-语义基元，实现在非结构化自然环境中准确且泛化性强的全局定位，平均轨迹误差2.4米（19公里遍历）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS缺失环境中的可靠全局定位仍是一个未解决问题。现有基于航拍图像的方法主要针对结构化城市环境，难以处理自然户外区域中常见的重复几何结构和无特征地形，且依赖针对特定环境训练的模型。

Method: 提出Meridian方法，匹配航拍图像与地面RGB-D数据中的高层度量-语义基元；设计新的一致性度量指标估计机器人子图位姿分布；通过鲁棒位姿图优化剔除离群假设，实现精确轨迹估计。

Result: 在自动驾驶数据集、公园/校园区域及荒野营地等多种环境中验证，19公里地面遍历平均优化轨迹误差为2.4米。

Conclusion: 无需区域特定数据训练或算法微调即可实现跨多样化环境的准确全局定位，有效解决了非结构化自然地形中的定位难题。

Abstract: Successful robot automation requires accurate global localization to support repeatability, task planning, goal specification, and safe operation. However, reliable localization in GNSS-denied environments remains an open problem. Overhead aerial imagery offers a promising solution, but existing approaches primarily target structured urban environments and have been rarely demonstrated in unstructured natural terrain. Limitations of the state-of-the-art include a reliance on models trained for specific environments, as well as difficulty handling repetitive geometries and featureless landscapes commonly found in natural outdoor areas. To overcome these challenges, we present Meridian, a method for matching high-level metric-semantic primitives across aerial images and ground robot RGB-D camera data that achieves accurate global localization and generalizes well across diverse environments, all without any training or algorithmic fine-tuning on area-specific data. We formulate novel consistency metrics to estimate a distribution over robot submap poses and to reject outlier hypotheses in a robust pose graph optimization step for accurate robot trajectory estimation. We demonstrate that our algorithm can localize a ground robot across a wide variety of environments, including an autonomous driving dataset, a park and campus area, and a wilderness camp, with an average optimized trajectory error of 2.4 m over 19 km of ground traversal.

</details>


### [89] [VOLT: Vision and Language Trajectory Segmentation for Faster-than-Demonstration Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06323)
*Robert Ramirez Sanchez,Daniel J. Evans,Dylan P. Losey,Siddarth Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VOLT方法，通过视觉-语言轨迹分割识别演示中需要慢速精确操作的部分，仅对可加速片段进行下采样，使机器人能够学习快于演示的策略，在加速执行任务的同时保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示任务通常比机器人实际执行慢，而许多实际应用要求机器人尽可能快速完成任务。现有方法简单地对整个演示轨迹进行均匀下采样会导致问题，因为任务中某些部分（如物体交互、精细操作）需要慢速精确运动，而另一些部分（如无障碍运动）则可以安全加速。

Method: 提出VOLT（Vision-and-Language Trajectory segmentation），一种基于视觉和语言的轨迹分割方法，通过分析视频演示并利用上下文线索，识别哪些片段需要慢速精细运动，哪些片段可以安全加速。对可加速片段进行选择性下采样，生成重构后的轨迹，再结合标准模仿学习方法（如扩散策略）进行策略学习。

Result: 实验表明，分割质量至关重要——基线方法经常错误判断何时可以加速，导致策略过于保守或不可靠。与最先进方法相比，VOLT使机器人能够更快地执行任务，同时保持强性能。

Conclusion: VOLT通过智能轨迹分割实现了比演示更快的机器人执行策略，验证了选择性加速而非均匀下采样是更有效的策略学习方式。

Abstract: Humans often take longer to demonstrate a task than a robot would need to execute it. Rather than learning to replicate the demonstration at the same pace, many industrial and practical applications require robots to perform tasks as quickly as possible. In this paper, we investigate several hypotheses for learning policies that operate faster-than-demonstrations. Our experiments show that the most effective strategy is to downsample recorded demonstrations and train the robot's policy on this accelerated data. However, uniformly downsampling an entire trajectory can be problematic. Some parts of a task can be safely sped up (e.g., unconstrained motion), while others demand slower, more precise motion (e.g., object interactions or fine manipulation). To address this challenge, we introduce VOLT, a vision-and-language trajectory segmentation method that reasons over video demonstrations, and leverages contextual cues to determine when acceleration is appropriate and when careful precision is required. VOLT identifies segments where slow, deliberate motion is necessary, then selectively downsamples the remaining segments. The resulting reformatted trajectories can be used with standard imitation learning approaches, such as diffusion policies. Our results highlight that segmentation quality is critical -- baseline methods often misidentify when acceleration is possible, leading to overly cautious or unreliable policies. Compared to state-of-the-art alternatives, VOLT allows robots to execute tasks faster while maintaining strong performance.

</details>


### [90] [Waypoints Matter: A Systematic Study for Sampling-Based Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06366)
*Josep M. Barbera,Antonio Artuñedo,Jorge Villagra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了自动驾驶轨迹规划器中目标路径点放置策略对规划性能的影响，发现路径点间距$d_s$是主导性能参数，均匀采样在调优后优于或持平于几何感知策略，曲率自适应策略仅在复杂道路的可靠性优先场景下有微弱优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径点放置直接影响可行轨迹的存在性与质量，但此前其作为设计变量的影响未被充分探索。

Method: 固定轨迹基元与候选数，在449种配置和5张地图上系统对比三种放置策略：均匀间距、RDP*变体、以及新颖的曲率条件分配。

Result: 名义路径点间距$d_s$是主要性能驱动因素，均匀采样在调优后匹配或超越RDP*和曲率变体；曲率变体仅在几何复杂道路上可靠性优先时略有优势，RDP*从未优于均匀采样。

Conclusion: $d_s$应作为主导调优参数，几何感知策略仅保留用于曲率丰富且可行性受限的走廊场景。

Abstract: Real-time autonomous driving commonly relies on sampling-based trajectory planners that link candidate trajectories to target waypoints along the road centerline. The placement of these waypoints directly impacts both the existence and quality of feasible trajectories. Yet, its effect on planner performance remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we treat waypoint placement as a first-class design variable. We hold the trajectory primitive and candidate budget fixed, and systematically sweep three placement strategies (uniform spacing, an augmented Ramer-Douglas-Peucker variant (RDP*), and a novel curvature-conditioned allocation) across 449 configurations and five CommonRoad maps of increasing geometric complexity. Our results show that the nominal inter-waypoint spacing $d_s$ is the primary performance driver, with large differences in planner reliability attributed to placement alone. Uniform sampling at a well-tuned spacing matches or surpasses both RDP* and the centered curvature variant. The curvature variant offers a small but consistent advantage on geometrically complex roads under reliability-first and balanced weightings, while RDP* never outperforms uniform sampling. These findings suggest that $d_s$ should be treated as the dominant tuning parameter, with geometry-aware strategies reserved for curvature-rich corridors where feasibility is the limiting factor.

</details>


### [91] [Ensuring Interaction Safety in Multitask Exoskeleton Control: A Simulation-Trained Variable Impedance Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06370)
*Muyuan Ma,Houcheng Li,Haotian Zhai,Lijun Han,Xinpan Meng,Xiuze Xia,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于仿真训练的可变阻抗控制框架，结合PPO生成人体运动数据、多模态策略网络预测参考轨迹与阻抗参数，并利用李雅普诺夫稳定性理论约束输出保证安全，实验表明该方法在多任务外骨骼控制中有效降低代谢成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴外骨骼需要适应多样化任务同时保证交互安全，现有方法难以同时满足这两点要求。

Method: 1) 基于PPO的仿真人机运动数据生成管道；2) 融合语义指令与本体感受历史的双模态策略网络，预测参考轨迹和可变阻抗增益；3) 基于李雅普诺夫稳定性理论的输出约束，保证系统渐近稳定性。

Result: 在真实场景下相比基线方法降低了代谢成本，实现了安全的多任务外骨骼控制。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够在保证安全的前提下实现多任务外骨骼自适应控制，具有良好的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Wearable exoskeletons can augment human phys ical capabilities during complex activities. However, ensuring adaptation across diverse tasks while guaranteeing interaction safety remains a critical challenge. To address this, a simulation trained variable impedance control approach with stability guarantees is proposed. First, a simulation-based human exoskeleton motion data generation pipeline is established, utilizing Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) to synthesize human muscle activations while the exoskeleton provides direct compensation for human biological joint torques. Subsequently, the generated dataset is used to train a dual modality policy that fuses semantic instructions with proprioceptive history, enabling the prediction of reference trajectories and variable impedance gains for nine different motion tasks. To guarantee safety, the network outputs are constrained by a stability criterion derived from Lyapunov stability theory, which bounds stiffness variations to ensure the asymptotic stability of the coupled system. Experimental results indicate that the proposed framework reduces metabolic cost in real-world scenarios com pared with standard baseline methods. These findings suggest the feasibility of the proposed framework for safe, multitask exoskeleton control.

</details>


### [92] [RiskFlow: Fast and Faithful Safety-Critical Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06423)
*Qi Lan,Yining Tang,Yu Shen,Yi Zhou,Yuhao Wei,Jie Li,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RiskFlow提出一种基于动作空间传输的闭环安全关键多智能体交通生成框架，通过单次前向传播学习平均速度场将高斯动作序列转化为加速度和偏航率指令，避免迭代去噪的计算开销和累积误差，在保持对抗性的同时提升真实性和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的闭环生成方法计算成本高，且长 rollout 中迭代去噪会累积采样和引导误差，导致不真实运动伪影（抖动、异常加速、偏离道路）。

Method: RiskFlow 将未来轨迹生成建模为动作空间中的传输过程，学习有限区间上的平均速度场，通过单次前向传播将高斯动作序列转换为加速度和偏航率命令；训练采用 JVP 目标函数，测试时对生成动作施加输出空间引导以鼓励风险交互并约束离路行为，最后通过车辆动力学重建物理可行的轨迹。

Result: 在 nuScenes 数据集上进行 tbsim 闭环评估，RiskFlow 在多智能体和长时域设置中实现了对抗性与真实性的强平衡，相比代表性基线持续提升真实性，同时保持有竞争力的安全关键生成能力，并大幅减少推理时间。

Conclusion: RiskFlow 通过动作空间传输替代迭代去噪，有效缓解了扩散方法的计算开销和采样误差累积问题，实现了高效、真实且可引导的安全关键交通场景生成。

Abstract: Safety-critical traffic scenario generation is essential for evaluating autonomous driving systems under rare but high-risk interactions. Existing diffusion-based methods offer strong controllability in closed-loop generation, but their iterative denoising process is computationally expensive and may accumulate sampling and guidance errors over long rollouts, causing unrealistic motion artifacts such as jitter, abnormal acceleration, and off-road behavior. To address these issues, we propose RiskFlow, a closed-loop safety-critical multi-agent traffic generation framework that formulates future trajectory generation as transport in the action space. Instead of relying on iterative denoising, RiskFlow learns an average velocity field over a finite interval to transform Gaussian action sequences into future acceleration and yaw-rate commands with a single forward pass, using a JVP-based objective for efficient and stable training. At test time, RiskFlow applies output-space guidance to the generated actions, steering selected critical agents toward risky interactions while regularizing off-road behavior, and reconstructs physically feasible trajectories through vehicle dynamics. Experiments on nuScenes with tbsim closed-loop evaluation show that RiskFlow achieves a strong adversariality-realism trade-off across multi-agent and long-horizon settings. Compared with representative baselines, RiskFlow consistently improves realism while maintaining competitive safety-critical generation capability, and substantially reduces inference time for evaluation.

</details>


### [93] [Flow-based Policy Adaptation without Policy Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06461)
*Luzhe Sun,Jingtian Ji,Haoran Chen,Jiawei Zhou,Matthew R. Walter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GLOVES提出了一种基于流的动作适应方法，通过少量专家演示将非专家动作纠正为专家动作分布，并利用逆向流评分作为干预门控，仅在动作异常时进行修正，实现轻量级的共享控制模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从预训练策略、基础模型或人类操作中获取的动作为机器人技能学习提供了高效途径，但这些动作往往是次优、有噪声或与任务目标不一致的，需要一种轻量级方法来修正非专家动作，同时保留代理意图。

Method: 提出GLOVES，一种基于流的适应方法族。通过学习从非专家动作分布到专家动作分布的传输流（flow），并在推理时通过逆向流评估计算动作的分布内评分，以此作为干预门控信号：仅对离群或分布外动作进行修正，一致动作则直接通过。

Result: 该方法仅需少量专家演示或可复用的成功技能片段即可生效，通过学习局部专家动作模式并在执行时拼接，实现了跨任务和环境鲁棒的动作适应。

Conclusion: GLOVES提供了一种轻量级共享控制模块，能够有效改善任务成功率，同时保留原始代理的意图，仅在必要时提供辅助。

Abstract: Leveraging prior knowledge from pretrained policies, foundation models, or human operators offers an efficient alternative to learning robot skills from scratch. However, these agents often provide actions that are suboptimal, noisy, or misaligned with task-specific expert behavior. We propose GLOVES, a family of flow-based adaptation methods that correct non-expert actions by transporting them toward an expert action distribution. Rather than replacing agentic control with full autonomy, GLOVES performs selective action-level adaptation, improving task success while preserving agent intent. The learned flow also provides a natural in-distribution scoring mechanism through reverse flow evaluation. We use this signal as an intervention gate: actions that appear consistent with the expert distribution are passed through unchanged, while anomalous or out-of-distribution (OOD) actions are corrected. In this way, assistance is only provided when necessary. GLOVES requires only limited expert supervision, using a small number of demonstrations or reusable successful skill segments. By learning local expert action patterns and stitching them during execution, GLOVES provides a lightweight shared-control module for robust action adaptation across tasks and environments. Code and demos are available at ripl.github.io/GLOVES_web.

</details>


### [94] [TempoVLA: Learning Speed-Controllable Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06491)
*Dong Jing,Jingchen Nie,Tianqi Zhang,Jiaqi Liu,Huaxiu Yao,Zhiwu Lu,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TempoVLA提出了一种通过显式速度条件控制VLA执行速度的方法，利用变速度轨迹增强（VSTA）和数据侧条件机制，实现机器人操作中低风险阶段加速、高风险阶段减速的灵活速度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型仅继承训练示范的单一固定速度，加速方法（模型压缩、KV缓存复用、强化学习）只能将策略从一种固定速度迁移至另一种，且几乎未探索减速控制。

Method: (1) 数据侧：变速度轨迹增强（VSTA），通过合并或分割动作将示范重新计时到任意目标速度，同时保持运动语义；(2) 模型侧：将速度作为显式条件输入策略网络。

Result: VSTA在达到目标速度时运动误差可忽略；仿真和真实任务中TempoVLA实现双向灵活速度控制；VSTA通过更好的数据利用提升默认1倍性能；与大模型协作实现动态速度控制。

Conclusion: 通过动作幅度的显式速度条件控制，可以突破VLA固定速度限制，实现低风险阶段加速、高风险阶段减速的灵活且安全的机器人操作。

Abstract: Robot manipulation alternates between low-risk transit phases that call for fast execution and high-risk contact stages that demand slow, precise motion. Yet existing Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) only inherit a single fixed speed from training demonstrations. Prior efforts to accelerate VLAs through model compression, KV-cache reuse, or reinforcement learning only shift the policy from one fixed speed to another, and leave deceleration almost unexplored. We observe that the magnitude of each predicted action already governs how fast the robot moves, opening a direct route to controllable execution speed. We turn this observation into TempoVLA, a single VLA whose execution speed is controlled by an explicit condition. TempoVLA combines two coupled components. (1) A data-side Variable-Speed Trajectory Augmentation (VSTA) that re-times demonstration to any target speed by merging or splitting actions while preserving its motion semantics. (2) A model-side conditioning mechanism that feeds the speed to the policy. Statistics show that VSTA reaches the requested speed with negligible motion error. Experiments in simulation and on real-world tasks demonstrate that TempoVLA achieves flexible speed control in both directions, while VSTA additionally boosts the default $1\times$ performance via better data utilization. Furthermore, by cooperating with a large multimodal model, TempoVLA realizes dynamic speed control, accelerating through low-risk phases and decelerating for high-risk ones.

</details>


### [95] [HANDOFF: Humanoid Agentic Task-Space Whole-Body Control via Distilled Complementary Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06493)
*Lizhi Yang,Junheng Li,Nehar Poddar,Yiling Hou,Gio Huh,Robert Griffin,Georgia Gkioxari,Aaron Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HANDOFF——一种面向人形机器人的紧凑型全身控制接口，通过多教师知识蒸馏与混合专家模型，实现从自然语言到全身动作的泛化控制，在Unitree G1上展示了与SOTA匹配的速度跟踪性能和最大的鲁棒操作工作空间之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身控制器需要稠密的运动学或空间参考信号，任务规划器难以从任务语义中合成这类信号，因此亟需一种既直观通用又足够表达多样化操作技能的紧凑接口。

Method: 提出HANDOFF接口（紧凑显式命令空间），通过上下文条件门控机制的多教师KL散度蒸馏，将三个互补专家（全身运动跟踪+安全滤波数据、 locomotion、跌倒恢复）蒸馏为混合专家学生模型。

Result: 在Unitree G1上，HANDOFF的速度跟踪达到SOTA水平，提供了最大鲁棒操作工作空间之一，并通过VLM驱动的智能规划器实现了多轮自然语言任务部署。

Conclusion: HANDOFF验证了紧凑接口+多教师蒸馏范式在人形机器人全身控制中的有效性，无需任务特定数据或控制器微调即可泛化到自然语言驱动的操作任务。

Abstract: For a humanoid robot to be deployed in the real world, the choice of command space (i.e., the interface between task planning and whole-body control) is crucial. Existing whole-body controllers typically demand dense kinematic or spatial references that planners struggle to synthesize from task semantics. We instead propose a compact, explicit interface that is intuitive, general, modular, and expressive enough for diverse manipulation skills. To this end, we introduce HANDOFF, a single humanoid whole-body controller that follows this interface and is distilled via multi-teacher KL distillation under a context-conditioned gating scheme into a mixture-of-experts student from three complementary specialists: whole-body motion tracking with safety-filtered data, locomotion, and fall-recovery. On the Unitree G1, HANDOFF matches state-of-the-art velocity tracking and offers one of the largest robust manipulation workspaces. We further demonstrate hardware feasibility through multiple natural-language-driven task roll-outs, powered by a VLM-driven agentic planner with no task-specific data or controller fine-tuning.

</details>

### [96] [Euler Scheme for Stochastic Functional Differential Equations Driven by Fractional Brownian Motion via Fractional Calculus Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05195)
*Alexander Abreu,Héctor Araya,Lisandro Fermin,Johanna Garzón,Soledad Torres*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类由Hurst参数H>1/2的分数布朗运动驱动的带记忆的随机泛函微分方程，提出并分析了欧拉型数值格式，证明了收敛性并推导了离散化步长对应的收敛速率，数值模拟验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数布朗运动可以捕捉时间序列中的长程依赖性，而带记忆的随机泛函微分方程能刻画更丰富的动力学行为。然而，此类方程的数值求解方法尚不充分，现有方案缺乏严格的理论保证。本文旨在填补这一空白，为相关应用领域提供可靠的数值工具。

Method: 针对Hurst参数H>1/2的分数布朗运动驱动的随机泛函微分方程，构造了欧拉型数值格式。利用分数阶微积分工具，在漂移和扩散系数满足适当正则性条件下，分析了该格式的收敛性，并推导出收敛速率关于离散化步长的显式表达式。

Result: 严格证明了所提欧拉格式的收敛性，得到了与离散化步长相关的收敛速率；数值模拟结果与理论分析一致，验证了方法的准确性。

Conclusion: 所提出的欧拉型数值格式对于分数布朗运动驱动的带记忆随机泛函微分方程是有效且收敛的，理论速率在实际计算中得到了验证。该工作为更复杂分数阶随机系统的数值模拟奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study a stochastic functional differential equation (SFDE) with memory driven by a fractional Brownian motion (fBm) with Hurst parameter H>1/2. An Euler-type numerical scheme is proposed and analyzed under suitable regularity conditions on the drift and diffusion coefficients using tools from fractional calculus. We prove the convergence of the scheme and derive the corresponding rate in terms of the discretization step. Numerical simulations illustrate the theoretical results and confirm the accuracy of the proposed method.

</details>


### [97] [Optimizing Irreversible Perturbations of the Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05324)
*Qianyu Julie Zhu,Youssef Marzouk,Konstantinos Spiliopoulos,Benjamin Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对非高斯目标分布和时间离散化对Langevin动力学中不可逆扰动设计的影响，提出了一个系统框架来优化ULA算法中的位置无关不可逆扰动，该框架同时考虑混合效率（通过谱间隙表征）和离散化偏差（通过加权期望平方跳距离量化），并给出了最优扰动的显式刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间高斯设定下的最优不可逆扰动设计已有研究，但扩展到非高斯目标分布以及时间离散化对最优扰动设计的影响尚未被充分理解；数值离散化引入的偏差通常会被不可逆扰动加剧，需要联合处理加速与精度问题。

Method: 构建了一个约束优化问题，同时考虑混合效率（由谱间隙类似量表征）和离散化偏差（由加权期望平方跳距离量化），并在此框架下推导了最优位置无关不可逆扰动的显式表达式。

Result: 所设计的最优扰动在数值实验中展现出更快的收敛速度且偏差可控，相较于其他不可逆扰动选择，均方估计误差显著降低。

Conclusion: 本文建立的联合优化框架有效解决了不可逆扰动在加速Langevin动力学中与离散化偏差之间的权衡问题，为实际应用中的扰动设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Irreversible perturbations accelerate the convergence of Langevin dynamics, breaking detailed balance while preserving the invariant measure. The design of optimal irreversible perturbations has been studied in the continuous-time Gaussian setting, but extensions to non-Gaussian target distributions, and the impact of time discretization on the design of optimal perturbations, have not been well understood. Numerical discretizations of Langevin dynamics introduce bias, which is typically exacerbated by irreversible perturbations; handling this interaction demands a joint treatment of acceleration and accuracy. This paper develops a systematic framework for optimizing position-independent irreversible perturbations of the unadjusted Langevin algorithm (ULA). We formulate a constrained optimization problem that simultaneously accounts for mixing efficiency and discretization bias, where the former is characterized by a spectral gap analogue and the latter is quantified via a weighted expected squared jump distance. Within this framework, we derive an explicit characterization of the optimal position-independent irreversible perturbation. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate that our design yields faster convergence with controlled bias, and improves mean squared estimation errors compared to other choices of irreversible perturbation.

</details>


### [98] [Entropy-Compatible Barrier Schemes for Diffusive FENE Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05209)
*Sai Peng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对FENE型构象张量模型中的有限可扩展性约束，开发了一种势垒保持的熵相容离散化方法，结合对数参数化、熵相容重构、分子扩散等技术，在理论上证明了有限可扩展性保持、自由能不等式、AP应力闭合等性质，并通过数值实验验证了方法的鲁棒性和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Oldroyd-B模型缺乏有限可扩展性约束，而FENE型模型要求构象张量满足tr C < L²。正定性不足以保证该约束，数值状态可能越过奇异迹势垒。即使采用迹保持的对数参数化，高阶重构仍可能在有限可扩展域内注入人工FENE熵。因此需要开发一种能严格保持迹势垒的数值方法。

Method: 提出了一种势垒保持的熵相容离散化方法，包含：(1) 迹势垒自由能；(2) 有限可扩展性对数参数化；(3) 最小阻尼熵相容势垒-对数重构；(4) 与势垒熵变量配对的分子扩散；(5) 聚合物功的相容求积；(6) 小Weissenberg数下的缩放FENE应力变量。

Result: 理论上证明了：有限可扩展性在熵求积点处的保持、最大熵容许重构参数的存在性和二分法可计算性、带松弛和分子扩散势垒耗散的全离散自由能不等式、定量AP应力闭合、固定离散化下的牛顿极限。导出了有限可扩展域紧子集上的条件相对熵估计。数值实验验证了势垒保持、熵相容重构、能量衰减、AP闭合、速度-压力-应力耦合精度以及迹约束附近的高Weissenberg数鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的势垒保持熵相容离散化方法能有效处理FENE型模型的有限可扩展性约束，在理论和数值上均证明了其可靠性，适用于FENE-P型流动及具有相同熵结构的迹奇异FENE族闭合模型。

Abstract: FENE-type conformation-tensor models impose a finite-extensibility constraint that is absent from Oldroyd--B flow: the conformation tensor must satisfy $\CC\succ0$ and $\tr\CC<L^2$. Positive definiteness alone is therefore insufficient, since a numerical state can remain positive while crossing the singular trace barrier. Even a trace-preserving logarithmic parametrization is not enough by itself: high-order reconstruction can remain inside the finite-extensibility domain while injecting artificial FENE entropy. We develop and analyze a barrier-preserving entropy-compatible discretization for FENE-P type flows with polymer center-of-mass molecular diffusion and for trace-singular FENE-family closures with the same entropy structure. The method combines a trace-barrier free energy, a finite-extensibility logarithmic parametrization, a least-damping entropy-compatible barrier-log reconstruction, molecular diffusion paired with the barrier entropy variable, compatible quadrature for polymeric work, and a scaled FENE stress variable for the small-Weissenberg limit. For admissible discrete states we prove finite-extensibility preservation at entropy quadrature points, existence and bisection computability of the maximal entropy-admissible reconstruction parameter, a fully discrete free-energy inequality with relaxation and molecular-diffusion barrier dissipation, a quantitative AP stress closure, and a fixed-discretization Newtonian limit. A conditional relative-entropy estimate is derived on compact subsets of the finite-extensibility domain. Numerical diagnostics verify barrier preservation, entropy-compatible reconstruction, energy decay, AP closure, coupled velocity--pressure--stress accuracy, and high-Weissenberg robustness near the trace constraint.

</details>


### [99] [Infinite sequences with optimal diaphony, periodic $L_2$-discrepancy, and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05482)
*Peter Kritzer,Nicolas Nagel,Friedrich Pillichshammer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明基于 $\mathbb{F}_2$ 的无限二阶数字序列在周期 $L_2$-差异和声响度上达到最优阶 $O((\log N)^{d/2}/N)$，将此前五阶构造的维度从 $5d$ 降至 $2d$，并在周期 Besov 空间中建立了拟蒙特卡洛积分的误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证无限二阶数字序列在周期 $L_2$-差异（声响度）上的最优性猜想，改进现有构造的实用性（降低维度），并在更广泛的 Besov 空间框架下建立拟蒙特卡洛积分误差界。

Method: 理论分析：证明基于 $\mathbb{F}_2$ 的无限二阶数字序列达到周期 $L_2$-差异的最优阶；将结果推广到周期 Besov 空间 $S_{p,q}^rB(\mathbb{T}^d)$，推导不同光滑参数 $r$ 下的最坏情况误差阶。

Result: 无限二阶数字序列在周期 $L_2$-差异上达到 $C_d (\log N)^{d/2}/N$ 的最优阶；在 Besov 空间中，当 $r\neq1$ 时误差阶为 $(\log N)^{(d-1)(1-1/q)}/N^{\min(r,1)}$，当 $r=1$ 时为 $(\log N)^{d(1-1/q)}/N$；维度从 $5d$ 降至 $2d$。

Conclusion: 证实了二阶数字序列在周期 $L_2$-差异下的最优性猜想，显著提升了构造的实用性，并为拟蒙特卡洛方法在周期 Besov 空间中的应用提供了更紧的理论保证。

Abstract: We investigate the periodic $L_2$-discrepancy of infinite sequences $§_d$ in $[0,1)^d$ and its analytic counterpart, the diaphony. We prove that infinite order-2 digital sequences over $\mathbb{F}_2$ attain the optimal order $L_{2,N}^{\rm per}(§_d) \le C_d (\log N)^{d/2}/N$ for all $N \in \mathbb{N}\setminus \{1\}$, matching known lower bounds for infinitely many $N \in \mathbb{N}$. This confirms the conjectured optimality of order-2 constructions. By this result, we improve upon previously known constructions using order-5 digital sequences, and reduce the underlying dimension for the interlacing construction from $5d$ to $2d$, significantly improving practicality. We establish our bounds within a broader framework of quasi-Monte Carlo integration for periodic Besov spaces $S_{p,q}^rB(\mathbb{T}^d)$ with dominating mixed smoothness $r \in (1/p,2)$, where $p,q\in [1,\infty]$. Rules based on infinite order-2 digital sequences yield worst-case errors of order $(\log N)^{(d-1)(1-1/q)} / N^{ \min(r,1)}$ for $r \not=1$, and $(\log N)^{d(1-1/q)}/N$ for $r=1$, for all $N \in \mathbb{N}\setminus\{1\}$, while preserving extensibility in $N$.

</details>


### [100] [Weighted hp-Uniform Decompositions for H^k-Type Tensor-Product Spaces in Arbitrary Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05615)
*Situan Li,Weiying Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文建立了任意空间维度 d ≥ 1 下，针对 H^k 型协调与非协调张量积离散化在拟合界面网格上的加权 hp-均匀顶点-斑块分解，其常数与网格尺寸、多项式次数、相邻次数比及全局系数对比无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在界面网格上具有大系数跳跃和变次数多项式离散的H^k型空间中，稳定分解的存在性问题，消除对网格尺寸、多项式次数及系数对比的依赖性。

Method: 结合Hermite端点变换（用于0,...,k-1阶端点jets）、张量积扩展、加权破碎斑块Poincaré不等式以及法向导数跳跃的逐次校正。

Result: 在局部系数振荡界和局部高侧连通性条件下，协调H^k空间和非协调空间V_h^{(s)}（0≤s≤k）均具有稳定分解，常数仅依赖于d和k，不依赖于网格尺寸、多项式次数、相邻次数比及全局系数对比。k=1时结论可推广到均匀正则映射立方网格。

Conclusion: 该分解方法对于三维DG问题（大系数跳跃和强变化局部多项式次数）具有鲁棒性，数值实验支持理论预测的稳定性。

Abstract: We establish weighted hp-uniform vertex-patch decompositions in arbitrary space dimension d >= 1 for tensor-product discretizations of H^k-type conforming and nonconforming spaces, with arbitrary fixed Sobolev order k >= 1, on fitted interface meshes. The cells are coordinate-compatible cuboids, the local spaces are Q_{p_K}(K) with arbitrary elementwise degrees satisfying p_K >= 2k-1, and the coefficient may have arbitrarily large jumps across material interfaces. Under local coefficient oscillation bounds and a local high-side connectivity condition, both the conforming H^k space and the nonconforming spaces V_h^{(s)}, 0 <= s <= k, admit stable decompositions with constants which may depend on the fixed parameters d and k, but are independent of the mesh size, all polynomial degrees, neighboring degree ratios, and the global coefficient contrast. The argument combines a Hermite endpoint transform for endpoint jets of order 0,...,k-1, its tensor-product extension, weighted broken patch Poincare inequalities, and a successive correction of normal derivative jumps. Numerical experiments for a three-dimensional DG problem with large coefficient jumps and strongly varying local polynomial degrees support the predicted robustness. For k = 1 the same conclusions hold on uniformly regular mapped cubical meshes whose neighboring element maps agree on each common face.

</details>


### [101] [A Low-rank Interpolatory Projection Algorithm for Solving Large-scale T-Sylvester Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05640)
*Umair Zulfiqar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模T-Sylvester方程提出了一种基于切向内插的迭代投影算法，通过斜投影将问题转化为切向内插问题，并在无需先验信息的情况下迭代优化插值数据。数值实验表明，该方法在投影矩阵列数远少于现有Krylov子空间方法的情况下即可收敛，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模T-Sylvester方程（$AX - X^\top E^\top + B_1B_2^\top = 0$）在控制理论等领域有重要应用，其低秩解的存在性为高效求解提供了可能。然而，现有Krylov子空间投影方法需要大量投影维度才能收敛，计算代价高昂，因此需要更高效的算法。

Method: 首先证明当方程存在唯一低秩解时，问题可通过斜投影自然转化为切向内插问题。由于具体的插值点和切向方向先验未知，提出一种迭代插值投影算法：每次迭代中，根据当前插值条件构造投影矩阵，求解降阶方程得到近似解，然后利用残差信息更新插值数据，同时投影矩阵的列数逐步扩展，直至收敛。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提算法在投影矩阵列数远小于Krylov子空间方法的情况下即可收敛，验证了算法的优越性。

Conclusion: 提出的迭代插值投影算法能够高效求解大规模T-Sylvester方程的低秩解，在计算效率上显著优于现有的Krylov子空间方法。

Abstract: This paper considers large-scale T-Sylvester equations of the form $AX - X^\top E^\top + B_1B_2^\top = 0$, which admit a low-rank solution. It is shown that when the unique solution of the T-Sylvester equation is low-rank, the problem naturally reduces to a tangential interpolation problem via oblique projection. The specific interpolation points and tangential directions needed to obtain the low-rank solution are not known a priori, thus requiring an iterative approach. An iterative interpolatory projection algorithm is proposed based on these interpolation conditions, which iteratively refines the interpolation data as the projection matrices expand in the number of columns. Numerical examples demonstrate that the proposed algorithm converges with projection matrices having significantly fewer columns compared to existing Krylov-subspace-based projection methods, confirming the superiority of the proposed algorithm over existing approaches.

</details>


### [102] [Iterative Thresholding Pursuit with Continuation for $\ell_{1-2}$-Regularized Sparse Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05657)
*Junxi Wu,Zeyu Dong,Jun-Feng Yin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合阈值寻踪与限制性最小二乘更新步骤的ITP-C方法，用于ℓ1-2正则化稀疏恢复，具有下降保持和收敛保证，在合成和图像重建任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ℓ1-2迭代阈值方法仅为一阶方法，收敛速度受限且恢复精度有待提升。本文旨在设计一种超越一阶阈值的、兼具活跃集识别与二阶更新能力的稀疏恢复方法。

Method: 提出ITP-C方法：(1) 利用ℓ1-2近端步进行活跃集识别；(2) 在识别出的支撑集上执行限制性最小二乘追踪步（二阶更新）；(3) 引入严格下降检查以控制追踪步的不稳定性；(4) 采用连续化策略保持下降结构。

Result: 在合成稀疏恢复和图像重建数值实验中，ITP-C方法相比现有最优基线方法展现出更优的恢复性能，且提出的安全机制有效保持了下降特性。

Conclusion: 在Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz框架下证明了生成序列的收敛性，并证明了正确支撑集识别后的局部预言性性质，表明该方法在理论上和实验上均具有优势。

Abstract: Sparse recovery aims to reconstruct sparse signals from underdetermined and possibly noisy linear measurements. Existing $\ell_{1-2}$ iterative thresholding schemes are first-order methods. We propose an iterative thresholding pursuit method with continuation (ITP-C) for $\ell_{1-2}$-regularized sparse recovery. The method goes beyond first-order thresholding by combining the active-set identification capability of the $\ell_{1-2}$ proximal step with a restricted least-squares pursuit step that provides a second-order update on the identified support. The support is generated adaptively by the thresholding update, and no prior knowledge of the true sparsity level is required. To control the possible instability of the pursuit step while preserving the descent structure of the continuation scheme, we impose a strict descent check with respect to the dynamic objective. We establish convergence of the generated sequence under the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz framework and prove a local oracle-type property after correct support identification. Numerical experiments on synthetic sparse recovery and image reconstruction illustrate the descent preservation of the proposed safeguard and demonstrate the improved recovery performance of ITP-C over the state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [103] [Auxiliary Gradient-Flow Solvers for Generalized Newtonian Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05926)
*Stefano Zampini,Daniele Boffi,Gurt Dovletov,Peter Markowich*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种辅助梯度流框架，用于处理由N-函数控制的广义牛顿变分问题。通过引入辅助标量变量替代梯度（或对称梯度）的平方模量，将非线性从状态方程转移到辅助变量，得到一系列一致椭圆加权线性问题。在连续层面构造了适应N-函数增长的辅助能量，证明了下半连续性、测地λ-凸性及最小移动格式的指数收敛性。在有限元层面推导了Riesz映射下的度量梯度流，证明了半离散ODE的整体适定性，并建立了向广义牛顿能量欧拉-拉格朗日方程有限元解的收敛性。对于p-Laplacian和p-Stokes模型，在4/3≤p≤4且p≠2范围内给出了严格收敛结果，并提供了该范围外的渐近率估计。提出了包括算子分裂（Kac迭代作为特例）和自适应伪瞬态方法在内的实用时间离散格式。数值实验展示了鲁棒性、网格无关迭代次数，性能达到或超越牛顿法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理广义牛顿变分问题时，非线性本构关系对梯度（或对称梯度）的依赖导致直接求解困难。旨在通过引入辅助变量将非线性从状态方程剥离，转化为一系列线性子问题，从而简化计算并保证收敛性。

Method: 提出辅助梯度流框架：引入辅助标量变量表示梯度平方模量，将原非线性问题转化为加权线性问题的序列。在连续层面定义度量空间上的辅助能量，利用最小移动方案求解；在有限元层面通过显式Riesz映射构造度量梯度流，并设计算子分裂（包含Kac迭代）和自适应伪瞬态两种时间离散格式。

Result: 在N-函数控制的度量拓扑下证明了辅助能量的下半连续性和测地λ-凸性，以及最小移动格式的指数收敛性。半离散ODE整体适定，有限元解收敛到原问题解。对p-Laplacian/p-Stokes模型，在4/3≤p≤4且p≠2范围内给出严格收敛证明，范围外提供渐近率估计。数值实验在幂律、Carreau-Yasuda、正则化Bingham及最优设计模型中展示了鲁棒性和网格无关迭代次数，性能匹配或优于牛顿法。

Conclusion: 辅助梯度流框架为广义牛顿问题提供了一种兼具理论严谨性和计算高效性的新范式，将非线性问题转化为线性子问题序列，同时保证收敛性。所提时间离散格式（尤其是Kac迭代特例）和自适应伪瞬态方法具有实用价值，数值性能优异。

Abstract: We introduce an auxiliary gradient-flow framework for variational problems with generalized Newtonian structure governed by an N-function. The key idea is to replace the nonlinear constitutive dependence on the gradient, or symmetric gradient, by an auxiliary scalar variable representing its squared magnitude. This shifts the nonlinearity from the state equation to the auxiliary variable, yielding a sequence of uniformly elliptic weighted linear problems.
  At the continuous level, we construct an auxiliary energy on a metric space adapted to the growth of the underlying N-function. In this topology, we prove lower semicontinuity, geodesic $λ$-convexity, and exponential convergence of the associated minimizing-movement scheme. At the finite element level, we derive a metric gradient flow through an explicit Riesz map, prove global well-posedness of the resulting semi-discrete ODE, and establish convergence to the finite element solution of the Euler--Lagrange equations of the generalized Newtonian energy. For the $p$-Laplacian and $p$-Stokes models, this gives a rigorous convergence result for $4/3\le p\le 4$, $p\ne2$, with asymptotic rate estimates beyond this range.
  We also propose practical time discretizations, including an operator-splitting scheme that gives the \kac iteration as a special case, and an adaptive pseudo-transient method that can be implemented using scalable linear solvers. Numerical experiments for power-law, Carreau--Yasuda, regularized Bingham, and optimal-design models demonstrate robustness, mesh-independent iteration counts in the tested regimes, and performance that matches or outperforms Newton's method.

</details>


### [104] [Direct reconstruction for acoustic inverse Born scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05977)
*Nuutti Hyvönen,Lisa Schätzle*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对二维Helmholtz方程的逆介质散射问题，在Born近似下利用三角Zernike分解推导出显式重建公式，将对比度与远场数据的展开系数关联为可独立求解的三角系统，并通过数值实验证明该方法结合正则化后对弱散射及超越Born近似的非线性问题均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆介质散射问题虽然唯一可解，但严重病态且非线性，现有方法难以高效求解。本文希望在线性化框架下推导显式解析重建公式，克服非线性和病态性双重困难。

Method: 在Born近似下将对比度与远场数据线性化；借鉴电阻抗成像中的三角Zernike分解方法，选择合适的展开函数，使系统矩阵解耦为多个独立的无限三角系统；每个三角系统可通过前向代入独立求解；结合正则化方法处理病态性。

Result: 推导出对比度展开系数与远场数据展开系数之间的显式重建公式；系统矩阵解耦为空间角频率对应的独立三角系统，可通过前向代入高效求解；数值实验表明该方法在弱散射（Born近似范围内）及超越Born近似的非线性情形下均有效。

Conclusion: 三角Zernike分解结合Born近似为逆介质散射问题提供了一种有效的显式重建方法，且该方法对一定程度的非线性远场数据也具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We consider the inverse medium scattering problem for the Helmholtz equation in two dimensions, i.e., the task to recover a compactly supported penetrable two-dimensional scatterer from full knowledge of the associated far field data or, equivalently, the far field operator. Although this problem is uniquely solvable, it is severely ill-posed and nonlinear. In the regime of weak scattering, the Born approximation yields a linearized relation between the contrast and the far field data, thus overcoming the second difficulty. This linear setting allows to build on recent work on linearized electrical impedance tomography, which relies on triangular Zernike decompositions, to derive an explicit reconstruction formula that expresses the expansion coefficients of the contrast in terms of those of the far field data. By choosing the expansion functions appropriately, the resulting system matrix decouples into separate (infinite) triangular systems for the spatial angular frequencies in the contrast. Consequently, each of these systems can be solved independently by performing forward substitutions. Our numerical experiments indicate that this approach, combined with an adequate regularization method, remains effective even when applied to full nonlinear far field data beyond the Born regime.

</details>


### [105] [Learning solution operators of PDEs with sparse approximation methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06046)
*Sebastian Neumayer,Daniel Potts,Fabian Taubert*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合维度增量框架、乘积基展开与正交匹配追踪(OMP)的稀疏高维方法，用于逼近偏微分方程的解算子。与基于求积的稀疏逼近和傅里叶神经算子相比，该方法在保持竞争性精度的同时显著减少了所需PDE求解次数，且恢复的稀疏指标集可提供变量与参数交互的可解释性洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于求积的稀疏逼近方法需要大量PDE求解样本，计算成本高昂；而深度学习方法虽效率高但缺乏可解释性。本文旨在通过稀疏恢复技术降低样本复杂度，同时在精度和可解释性上取得优势。

Method: 采用维度增量框架，将解算子用乘积基展开表示，并利用正交匹配追踪(OMP)进行稀疏恢复，从而大幅减少所需样本量。与求积基稀疏逼近和傅里叶神经算子进行对比评估。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提方法在保持竞争性精度的同时，显著减少了所需PDE求解次数。当解在所选基中具有稀疏表示时优势尤为明显。恢复的稀疏指标集提供了可解释的变量交互信息。

Conclusion: 结合维度增量与OMP的稀疏高维方法在PDE解算子逼近中，能有效降低样本复杂度并保持精度，同时具备可解释性，是一种有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: We investigate the approximation of solution operators for partial differential equations (PDEs) using sparse high-dimensional techniques. Building on a dimension-incremental framework, we combine product basis expansions with sparse recovery methods, specifically orthogonal matching pursuit (OMP), to substantially reduce the required sample size compared with a previously considered cubature-based approach. We evaluate the resulting method numerically on several examples, comparing it against both cubature-based sparse approximation and Fourier neural operators in terms of accuracy, runtime, and sample size. The experiments show that our approach considerably reduces the number of required PDE solves relative to its predecessor while maintaining competitive accuracy, particularly when the solution admits a sparse representation in the chosen basis. Furthermore, the recovered sparse index sets yield interpretable insights into the relevant variables and parameter interactions.

</details>


### [106] [Residual-based Kaczmarz methods for tensor linear equations with t-product](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06057)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出了基于残差的张量Kaczmarz方法，并进一步开发了带重球动量的加速残差Kaczmarz方法，两者均基于t-积运算框架。

Result: 理论证明了收敛性并给出了收敛率上界；数值实验表明所提方法比现有张量Kaczmarz方法更快。

Conclusion: 所提出的残差基张量Kaczmarz方法及其动量加速版本在求解t-积张量线性方程组方面高效且优于现有方法。

Abstract: Tensor linear systems widely arise from high-dimensional data mining and computing, for instance, natural language processing and machine learning. A class of residual-based tensor Kaczmarz method is proposed for tensor linear equations with t-product. Theoretical analyses prove the convergence and give an upper bound of the convergence rate of the proposed method. Furthermore, an accelerated residual-based Kaczmarz method with heavy ball momentum is developed. Numerical experiments verify the efficiency of the proposed methods and demonstrate that they are faster than the existing tensor Kaczmarz methods.

</details>


### [107] [Weak order one convergence of structure-preserving stochastic theta methods for stochastic differential algebraic equations with time-dependent singular matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06071)
*Caiyuan Zhu,Ziheng Chen,Lin Chen,Yiwei Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类含时变奇异矩阵的指标1随机微分代数方程的弱收敛阶，证明了结构保持的随机theta方法在θ∈(0,1]下具有弱一阶收敛性，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将常数奇异矩阵情形推广到时变奇异矩阵，同时保留维持约束所需的投影结构，研究结构保持数值方法的弱收敛阶。

Method: 利用精确解的指标1代数-微分分解，建立了约束保持单步逼近的抽象弱收敛定理，并将其应用于θ∈(0,1]的随机theta方法。

Result: 在全局Lipschitz、线性增长和适当光滑性假设下，证明了该方法是适定的、在所有时间层上保持代数约束，且具有弱一阶收敛性。

Conclusion: 数值实验验证了结构保持性质和理论收敛阶，表明该方法有效处理了时变奇异矩阵的指标1随机微分代数方程。

Abstract: This paper studies the weak convergence order of structure-preserving stochastic theta methods for a class of index-$1$ stochastic differential algebraic equations with time-dependent singular matrices. The singular matrix is allowed to vary in time but preserves a fixed differential-algebraic splitting, thereby extending the constant singular-matrix setting while retaining the projector structure required for constraint preservation. By exploiting the index-$1$ algebraic-differential decomposition of the exact solution, we establish an abstract weak convergence theorem for constraint-preserving one-step approximations and apply it to the stochastic theta method with $θ\in (0,1]$. Under global Lipschitz, linear growth, and suitable smoothness assumptions, the considered method is proved to be well posed, to preserve the algebraic constraints at all time levels, and to converge with weak order one. Numerical experiments are finally presented to confirm the structure-preserving property and the theoretical convergence order.

</details>


### [108] [Ensemble Kalman Inversion as an Inertial Interacting Particle System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06121)
