TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于惯性动力学的二阶粒子系统（重球形式的EKI），通过引入阻尼、吸引力与短程排斥力来防止集成协方差崩溃，并在线性反问题中分析了其均值和涨落动力学，证明了完全坍塌构型的线性不稳定性，并通过约束最优性条件描述了渐近平衡态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准集成卡尔曼反演（EKI）方法的连续时间形式存在集成协方差过早坍塌的问题，导致方法对初始集成敏感，需要引入机制来维持集成多样性。

Method: 提出二阶粒子系统：粒子按惯性动力学演化，结合Kalman型松弛力、阻尼、向集成均值的吸引力，以及短程排斥相互作用。该方法可解释为连续时间EKI的重球（heavy-ball）形式，并加入了竞争性的吸引-排斥机制。

Result: 在线性反问题中证明了完全坍塌构型是线性不稳定的；通过约束最优性条件刻画了渐近平衡态（在集成协方差保留的子空间上）；给出了指数衰减估计；数值实验验证了惯性和排斥对集成动力学的影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的二阶方法通过惯性机制和排斥相互作用有效抑制了集成坍塌，相比一阶EKI方法具有更优的动力学行为。

Abstract: Ensemble Kalman Inversion (EKI) is a derivative-free, ensemble-based method for inverse and optimization problems. Its continuous-time formulation can be interpreted as an interacting particle system driven by a Kalman-type preconditioned descent direction. A well-known limitation of this dynamics is the possible premature collapse of the covariance of the ensemble, which makes the method sensitive to the initial ensemble.
  We introduce a second-order particle system in which the particles evolve according to an inertial dynamics. The model combines a Kalman-type relaxation force with damping, attraction towards the ensemble mean, and a short-range repulsive interaction designed to counteract ensemble collapse. The resulting dynamics can be interpreted as a heavy-ball reformulation of continuous-time EKI enriched by competing attractive and repulsive mechanisms.
  For linear inverse problems, we analyze the induced mean and fluctuation dynamics and identify a parameter regime in which fully collapsed configurations are linearly unstable. We further characterize asymptotic equilibria through a constrained optimality condition on the subspace retained by the limiting ensemble covariance and derive an exponential decay estimate. Numerical experiments illustrate the effect of inertia and repulsion on the ensemble dynamics and compare the proposed second-order method with first-order EKI-type

</details>


### [109] [A tensor-train multidimensional inverse Laplace transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06093)
*Martin Mikkelsen,Michael Kastoryano*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量列（TT）格式的多维拉普拉斯逆变换方法，通过将变换函数在复求积网格上构造TT近似，再利用张量缩进行逆变换，将计算复杂度从指数级降至多项式级，并在数值实验中验证了其优于蒙特卡洛方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维拉普拉斯逆变换在应用数学、物理、金融和概率论中广泛存在，但传统数值求积方法会因维度增加导致求积节点数量指数增长，计算变得不可行，亟需一种能克服维度灾难的高效数值方法。

Method: 在复求积网格上构造被变换函数的张量列（TT）近似，然后通过一系列张量缩并完成逆变换。若相关键维数（bond dimensions）有界，计算成本从指数级降至多项式级。

Result: 该方法仅需少量可调参数，支持误差估计。对多元正态逆高斯分布、Wishart分布和相关Gamma型分布的数值实验表明，其性能优于蒙特卡洛估计，与精确参考值吻合良好。

Conclusion: 张量列格式能有效克服多维拉普拉斯逆变换的维度灾难，在低秩假设下实现高效、可扩展的计算，为高维概率分布和金融建模等应用提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Laplace transforms and their numerical inverses arise throughout applied mathematics, physics, finance, and probability theory. Numerical inversion, however, quickly becomes intractable in high dimensions because the number of quadrature evaluations grows exponentially with dimension. We develop a tensor train (TT) formulation of the multidimensional inverse Laplace transform. The method constructs a TT approximation of the transformed function on the complex quadrature grid and then performs the inversion through a sequence of tensor contractions. Under suitable low-rank assumptions, this reduces the computational cost from exponential to polynomial in the dimension, provided that the relevant bond dimensions remain bounded. The method has only a small number of tunable parameters and admits error estimations. We demonstrate its performance in numerical experiments, benchmarked against Monte Carlo estimates and exact references, for multivariate normal-inverse Gaussian, Wishart, and correlated Gamma-type distributions.

</details>


### [110] [A q-Tsallis Safe Approximation for Chance-Constrained Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06401)
*Sergio Assunção Monteiro,Fabricio Alves Barbosa da Silva*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Tsallis统计流形几何的q-CCP方法，通过秩基q-CVaR护航权重和测地线投影实现机会约束规划的严格紧化，在巴西Ibovespa股票和M5供应链库存实验中验证了理论优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典机会约束规划(CVaR-CCP)在重尾分布下系统性低估尾事件，需要一种更稳健的安全近似方法。

Method: 基于Tsallis统计流形的黎曼几何，引入q-CVaR护航权重作为尾单纯形面上的g^(q)-测地线投影，构建q-CCP可行集为Tsallis散度球，并提出迭代线性规划求解。

Result: q-CCP在q>1时严格收紧CVaR-CCP；经验违背比ρ(q)与尾指数ν无关；可行域体积成本随q和ν单调递增。Ibovespa实验违背比0.241，最优q=1.50；M5库存实验最优q=1.88，成本溢价1.155倍，零缺货违背。

Conclusion: q-CCP提供了数据自适应安全调节旋钮，继承了凸性和相干性，2-3次迭代即可收敛，在金融和供应链场景中有效性得到验证。

Abstract: Classical chance-constrained programs are solved by safe approximations based on the empirical CVaR, which uses a uniform measure over scenarios and systematically underweights tail events under heavy-tailed distributions. We introduce \emph{q-CCP}, a non-extensive safe approximation grounded in the Riemannian geometry of the Tsallis statistical manifold: the rank-based q-CVaR escort weights are the $g^{(q)}$-geodesic projection onto the tail simplex face, and the q-CCP feasible set is a Tsallis-divergence ball (Proposition~12). This geometric foundation yields three results. First, q-CCP is a provable strict tightening of CVaR-CCP for all $q > 1$ (Theorem~7). Second, the empirical violation ratio satisfies $ρ(q) = [1-(1-\varepsilon)^{q+1}]/\varepsilon$, independent of the tail index $ν$ (Proposition~10). Third, the feasible-region volume cost is monotone increasing in $q$ and $ν$ (Proposition~11), providing a data-adaptive safety knob. The formulation inherits convexity and coherence from the q-CVaR functional and admits an iterative LP reformulation converging in 2--3 iterations. Experiments on 15 Ibovespa equities confirm the theory (violation ratio $0.241$, $q^* = 1.50$); an M5 inventory newsvendor experiment generalises the method to supply chain ($q^* = 1.88$, cost premium $1.155\times$, zero OOS stockout violations).

</details>


### [111] [A Microlocal Open-Boundary Method for Residual-Based Wave Solvers on Unbounded Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06115)
*Avy Soffer,Nguyen Gia Hien,Minh-Binh Tran*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种微局域相空间滤波物理信息神经网络（PINN-TDPSF）框架，用于处理无界域上的波传播问题。该方法通过结合层状神经残差逼近和时相关相空间滤波，用相空间辐射机制替代传统的人工边界吸收条件，有效减少人工反射和环绕效应，适用于非线性、各向异性、稀疏数据等非FFT可对角化的物理场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工边界条件在处理无界域波传播时存在反射和环绕问题，而FFT、谱方法或分裂步进法仅适用于已知系数的正向问题。对于非线性残差、稀疏或非网格观测、未知系数、可变内部介质等复杂场景，需要一种更灵活的框架来减少人工边界反射并保持物理一致性。

Method: 采用"微局域相空间滤波物理信息神经网络（PINN-TDPSF）"框架：在物理计算域内部使用层状神经残差近似来逼近波的演化，在计算域周围设置缓冲区域，应用时相关相空间滤波器。只有当分量位于人工边界附近且其群速度指向外部时，才移除该分量，从而实现局部人工边界惩罚的替代。

Result: 在线性薛定谔传播、势散射、各向异性薛定谔动力学、非线性薛定谔波包、孤子应力测试、线性化欧拉波以及稀疏数据恢复局部声学缺陷等数值实验中，该方法有效减少了人工反射和环绕效应，能在各向异性介质中正确使用群速度，保留了物理上的入射分支分量，并在出射波包滤波假设被违反时提供诊断信息。

Conclusion: 该框架为无界域波传播问题提供了一种灵活的神经求解方案，特别适用于传统FFT类方法无法处理或难以应用的复杂物理场景，为开域波传播与非FFT可对角化物理的耦合提供了有效途径。

Abstract: We introduce a microlocal phase-space-filtered physics-informed neural network (PINN--TDPSF or Microlocal PINNFilter) framework for wave propagation on unbounded domains. The method combines a slabwise neural residual approximation of the interior evolution with a time-dependent phase-space filter applied in a buffer surrounding the physical computational domain. The central idea is to replace local artificial-boundary penalties by a phase-space radiation mechanism: a component is removed only when it is localized near the artificial boundary and its group velocity points outward.
  The proposed method is not intended to replace FFT, spectral, or split-step solvers for known-coefficient forward problems where such methods are available and highly accurate. Instead, it embeds the time-dependent phase-space filter into a residual-based neural framework. This coupling is useful when open-domain wave propagation must be combined with nonlinear residuals, sparse or off-grid observations, unknown coefficients, variable interior media, or other non-FFT-diagonalizable physics. Numerical experiments for linear Schrödinger propagation, potential scattering, anisotropic Schrödinger dynamics, nonlinear Schrödinger wave packets, soliton stress tests, linearized Euler waves, and sparse-data recovery of a localized acoustic defect show that the method reduces artificial reflection and wraparound, uses group velocity correctly in anisotropic media, preserves physically incoming branch components, and provides diagnostics when the assumptions behind outgoing-packet filtering are violated.

</details>


### [112] [Post-processed frozen-flow methods for the long time sampling of ergodic dynamics on Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06150)
*Adrien Busnot Laurent,Sébastien Macé*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黎曼流形上遍历随机微分方程（包括黎曼朗之万动力学）的新型内蕴逼近方法，仅依赖测地线、平行移动等自然几何运算，无需嵌入或坐标。该方法使用新型Lie-Butcher级数代数操作推导高阶条件，优先长时采样效率，在给定精度下成本优于传统外蕴和内蕴方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统外蕴方法（如惩罚法、投影法）依赖嵌入或坐标，在黎曼流形上处理SDE时存在局限性，亟需一种纯几何的内蕴方法以实现高效且高精度的不变测度采样。

Method: 提出仅依赖测度、平行移动等自然几何运算的内蕴数值方法；给出不变测度高阶精度判据；利用新型Lie-Butcher级数代数操作推导高阶条件；借鉴Leimkuhler-Matthews方法思想，优先长时采样效率而非有限时间精度。

Result: 给出了高阶精度判据，开发了专用于不变测度采样的内蕴数值方法，通过数值实验证明了在给定精度下成本优于先前的外蕴和内蕴方法。

Conclusion: 本文的内蕴方法在黎曼流形上的遍历SDE近似中，兼顾了几何自然性与计算效率，为长时采样提供了更优方案。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a novel intrinsic approach to the approximation of ergodic SDEs on Riemannian manifolds, which include Riemannian Langevin dynamics. In opposition to the standard extrinsic approaches such as penalization methods and projection methods, our methodology does not use embeddings or coordinates and only relies on natural geometric operations: geodesics, parallel transport,... We give a criterion for high order of accuracy for the invariant measure, develop new intrinsic numerical methods designed solely for sampling the invariant measure, and derive high order conditions using a new algebraic operation on exotic Lie-Butcher series. In the spirit of the Leimkuhler-Matthews method, our approach prioritizes long time sampling efficiency over finite time accuracy, and outperforms the previous extrinsic and intrinsic approaches in terms of cost for a given accuracy, which we illustrate with several numerical experiments.

</details>


### [113] [Structure-Preserving Operator Splitting via JR-Decomposition for Circuit Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06153)
*Andreas Bartel,Malak Diab*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在端口-哈密顿框架下研究改进节点分析（MNA）电路模型，针对标准JR分解在电路应用中的局限性，提出了增强型JR分解方法，并通过数值实验验证了其收敛性和结构保持特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准JR分解在电路模型（MNA）中的应用受到限制，需要一种针对电路特性定制的分解方法来实现能量共形分裂。

Method: 在端口-哈密顿框架下研究MNA电路模型，通过松弛标准JR分解的条件，提出增强型JR分解方法，使其特别适用于电路应用。

Result: 数值实验表明增强型JR分解方法具有良好的适用性、收敛性和结构保持特性。

Conclusion: 增强型JR分解成功克服了标准JR分解在电路模型中的应用限制，为电路系统的能量共形数值处理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We investigate circuit models, namely, modified nodal analysis (MNA) in the port-Hamiltonian framework. Based on this, the JR-decomposition for the numerical treatment would offer an energy conform splitting. However, for circuit models, the application of the standard JR-decomposition is restricted. To enable a JR-decomposition for MNA, we need to relax the decomposition. To this end, we introduce the enhanced JR-decomposition, which is particularly tailored to the application to circuits. We conclude with a numerical example that illustrates the applicability of the proposed approach as well as its convergence and structure-preserving properties.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum Algorithm for Nonlinear and Stochastic Homogenization via a Young-Measure based Linear Programming Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06165)
*Siqi Chen,Shi Jin,Lei Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文通过Young测度LP公式将非线性/随机均匀化问题提升为高维线性问题，利用量子LP求解器在确定性和随机性场景中分别实现多项式加速和平方根加速，数值实验验证了方法的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非线性/随机均匀化方法需要直接解析精细尺度振荡，计算成本高昂。作者旨在通过Young测度将非线性问题提升为高维线性问题，从而利用量子LP求解器的优势来加速计算。

Method: 将非线性/随机均匀化问题通过Young测度转化为线性规划（LP）公式，将微观尺度、梯度及随机变量作为独立变量，将原问题提升到更高维度。利用量子LP求解器处理这一大规模结构化LP问题。

Result: 确定性场景中，在中等均匀化精度需求下实现多项式量子加速；随机场景中，通过将所有随机实现编码到单个LP中，获得随随机变量数量增长的平方根缩减；在精细精度下，Young测度的正则性或稀疏性可进一步扩展优势。一维和二维数值实验验证了LP公式的正确性。

Conclusion: Young测度LP公式结合量子LP求解器为非线性/随机均匀化问题提供了有效的加速途径，量子优势在精度需求适中或随机变量较多时尤为显著。

Abstract: We study quantum algorithms for nonlinear and stochastic homogenization via a Young-measure based linear programming (LP) formulation, which lifts the nonlinear problem to a linear one in higher dimensions by treating the microscale, the gradient, and possible random variables as independent variables, thereby capturing effective macroscopic quantities without directly resolving fine-scale oscillations. The resulting LP is large but structured, and its high-dimensional nature creates regimes in which quantum LP solvers outperform direct classical solvers: in the deterministic setting, polynomial quantum speedup arises when moderate homogenized accuracy suffices; in the stochastic setting, encoding all random realizations simultaneously in a single LP yields a quantum square-root reduction in stochastic sampling cost that grows with the number of random variables. Regularity or sparsity of the Young measure may further extend these advantages to fine-scale accuracy. Numerical experiments on one- and two-dimensional benchmarks confirm the correctness of the Young-measure LP formulation.

</details>


### [115] [Error Analysis of Tr-PINNs Algorithm for 2D Incompressible Navier-Stokes Equations with Non-Homogeneous Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06268)
*Dongjie Liu,Xuebo Li,Rong Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对传统PINNs在处理非齐次边界条件时仅用L²范数约束导致边界模拟不准确的问题，本文提出Tr-PINNs方法，通过校正边界值误差来提升计算精度，并基于非齐次Stokes问题建立了算法误差分析，数值实验验证了其精度的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINNs在处理非齐次边界条件的Navier-Stokes方程时，仅使用L²范数约束边界项，这种单一的约束策略可能导致边界模拟不准确，进而降低预测精度。为此，本文旨在解决这一关键问题。

Method: 提出Tr-PINNs（校正边界值误差的改进物理信息神经网络），通过修正边界值误差来改进传统PINNs框架。基于非齐次Stokes问题的结果，建立了Tr-PINNs的算法误差分析。

Result: 数值实验表明，Tr-PINNs算法在计算精度上取得了显著提升，有效改善了传统PINNs因边界模拟不准确导致的精度下降问题。

Conclusion: 通过校正边界值误差的Tr-PINNs方法能够有效解决非齐次边界条件下的边界模拟不准确问题，显著提高预测精度，为PINNs处理复杂边界条件提供了有效改进方案。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have been widely applied to solve Navier-Stokes equations by enforcing outputs and gradients of deep models to satisfy target equations. However, conventional PINNs only constrain the boundary terms by means of the $L^2$-norm when addressing the equations with non-homogeneous boundary conditions. This single constraint strategy may cause inaccurate boundary simulation, further resulting in the decline of prediction accuracy. To resolve this critical issue, this paper proposes an improved physics-informed neural network by correcting the error of the boundary value, which is called Tr-PINNs. Based on the results of nonhomogeneous Stokes problem, the algorithm error analysis of Tr-PINNs is established. The efficacy of the Tr-PINNs algorithm is demonstrated via numerical experiments, which further demonstrate that the Tr-PINNs algorithm achieves a remarkable improvement in computational accuracy.

</details>


### [116] [DAS-PINNs for high-dimensional partial differential equations: extending deep adaptive sampling to spacetime domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06314)
*Anshima Singh,David J. Silvester*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对时间依赖高维PDE的深度自适应采样框架，利用归一化流网络根据PDE残差分布动态生成采样点，无需显式时间推进，在2至8维空间问题上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINNs在处理具有空间局部化和动态演化解的高维时空PDE时，均匀配点采样效率极低，亟需一种无需时间推进的、能自动追踪时空高残差区域的自适应采样方法。

Method: 将空间和时间视为统一域（无显式时间推进），使用归一化流神经网络学习PDE残差分布，据此生成集中于难以学习区域的新配点，实现纯残差驱动的时空自适应采样。

Result: 在从二维空间的尖锐运动特征到八维空间的局部化结构等一系列基准问题上验证了该方法的效果。

Conclusion: 该框架能自动识别和追踪时空中的高残差区域，无需传统自适应策略所需的时间推进或移动网格，为高维时间依赖PDE的PINNs求解提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Time-dependent high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs) with spatially localised and dynamically evolving solutions pose a fundamental challenge for physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), as uniform collocation sampling becomes increasingly ineffective in high-dimensional spatiotemporal domains. In this work, a deep adaptive sampling framework for PINNs is extended to the time-dependent setting by treating space and time as a unified domain without any explicit time marching. A normalising flow neural network model effectively learns the distribution induced by the PDE residual and generates new collocation points concentrated in regions where the solution is most difficult to learn. Unlike conventional adaptive strategies that require explicit time stepping or moving meshes, high-residual regions are automatically identified and tracked across both space and time, driven purely by the PDE residual distribution. The effectiveness of the proposed strategy is assessed on a range of benchmark problems, from sharp and moving features in two spatial dimensions to localised structures in up to eight spatial dimensions.

</details>

### [117] [Nonthermal Particle Acceleration by Magnetic Pumping in Pulsating Plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05286)
*Giuseppe Arrò,Vladimir Zhdankin,Fabio Bacchini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的“脉动盒”装置，通过全动理学模拟研究高β等离子体在压缩-膨胀循环中的粒子加速机制。结果表明，磁泵效应能高效加速粒子，产生带有幂律尾的非热能量分布。数值结果与作者推导的广义最大熵模型高度吻合，该模型将分布的幂律指数与注入能量联系起来。相关发现对理解空间和天体等离子体中高能粒子的起源具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高β等离子体在压缩-膨胀循环中的粒子加速机制，特别是磁泵效应对产生非热粒子能量分布的贡献，以及建立理论模型来定量描述这种加速过程。

Method: 采用全动理学模拟方法，构建了“脉动盒”（pulsating box）数值装置，在压缩-膨胀循环条件下研究高β等离子体中粒子的加速行为。同时推导了一个广义最大熵模型，将粒子能量分布的幂律指数与系统注入能量相关联。

Result: 磁泵效应能够高效加速粒子，产生具有幂律尾的非热能量分布；数值模拟结果与广义最大熵模型的理论预测高度一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解空间和天体等离子体中高能粒子的起源提供了新的理论框架和数值证据，磁泵效应可能是产生非热粒子的重要机制之一。

Abstract: We present a new "pulsating box" setup to investigate particle acceleration in high-beta plasmas undergoing compression-expansion cycles. Our fully kinetic simulations show that particles are efficiently accelerated by magnetic pumping, producing nonthermal energy distributions with power-law tails. Numerical results are in excellent agreement with a generalized maximum entropy model that we derive, linking the power-law index of distributions to the injected energy. Our results are relevant for understanding the origin of high-energy particles in space and astrophysical plasmas.

</details>


### [118] [Constraining the jet base emission of M87* with past and future Event Horizon Telescope observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05307)
*Noemi La Bella,Michael Janssen,Britton Jeter,Hendrik Müller,Bram Van de Berg,Hung-Yi Pu,Paul Tiede,Heino Falcke*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究利用合成数据检验了EHT在不同阵列配置下探测M87*喷流基座的能力。结果表明，2022年阵列配置显著提升了对弱喷流特征的探测灵敏度，并确定了可可靠恢复的喷流强度下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管M87*已知拥有相对论性喷流，但在事件视界尺度上探测其喷流基座发射仍具挑战。本研究旨在确定当前及近未来EHT阵列配置下，喷流发射能被可靠探测的最低强度阈值。

Method: 使用2021、2022及近未来三种EHT阵列配置生成合成数据，采用半解析吸积-喷流模型（喷流发射可独立调谐），通过正则化最大似然法和贝叶斯成像进行图像重构，利用流量密度恢复、图像保真度和不确定性图评估可探测性。

Result: 喷流可探测性强烈依赖于喷流强度、阵列配置和成像方法。2022年阵列因短基线覆盖改善，对微弱喷流特征的恢复能力显著提升。确定了可可靠恢复的喷流强度下限。当前EHT已对M87*视界尺度的弱喷流发射敏感。

Conclusion: 若内喷流贡献了不可分辨致密流量的显著部分，则在2021年后的观测中应可见；若未发现清晰喷流信号，则表明视界尺度喷流仅贡献一小部分致密发射。EHT的持续扩展将进一步改善此类探测能力。

Abstract: We investigate the detectability of the jet base of M87* at Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) observing frequencies. Although M87 is known to host a prominent relativistic jet, detecting jet emission close to the black hole at horizon scales remains challenging. Our goal is to determine the minimum jet intensity that can be reliably detected with the recent EHT array configurations. We use synthetic EHT data generated for three array configurations corresponding to the 2021 and 2022 observing campaigns and to a near future EHT campaign. As input models, we employ semi analytic accretion jet models in which the jet emission can be tuned independently of the accretion flow. The synthetic data are reconstructed with regularized maximum likelihood and Bayesian imaging. Jet detectability is assessed through flux density recovery, image fidelity, and uncertainty maps. We find that jet detectability strongly depends on the jet intensity, the array configuration, and imaging methodology. Using our analysis, we determine a lower limit on the jet intensity that can be reliably recovered. The 2022 EHT array configuration represents a significant improvement over earlier arrays, enabling a more robust reconstruction of faint jet features. Our results indicate that the current EHT array is already sensitive to weak jet emission at horizon scales in M87*. The improved short-baseline coverage introduced in 2022 makes faint inner jet features more easily detectable. If the inner jet contributes a significant fraction of the unresolved compact flux, it should become visible in post-2021 observations. On the other hand, if no clear jet signature is found, this would suggest that the horizon-scale jet contributes only a small part of the compact emission. The continued expansion of the EHT will further improve the detection of such jet emission in M87*.

</details>


### [119] [The WISSHFUL program: the highest redshift UFO discovered in a non-lensed QSO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05312)
*G. Lanzuisi,L. Borrelli,E. Piconcelli,G. Chartas,A. Luminari,J. Reeves,V. Braito,E. Bertola,S. Bianchi,A. Comastri,M. Brusa,C. Vignali,F. Vito,S. Marchesi,M. Cappi,M. Dadina,L. Zappacosta,A. Tortosa,M. Bischetti,G. Vietri,F. Salvestrini,G. Bruni,M. Fanelli,E. Kammoun,X. Zhao,G. Matzeu,F. Tombesi,A. Marinucci,M. Gaspari,T. Misawa,D. Stern*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: WISSHFUL项目首次公布了宇宙正午时期超爱丁顿吸积类星体WISSH13（红移z=3.294）的高质量宽带X射线光谱。光谱分析发现软光子指数（Γ~2）、强反射（R~1.4-1.8）、低高能截止（E_cut~60-80 keV），以及两个吸收特征（~7.5 keV和~10 keV），分别对应速度约0.1c和0.3c的超快外流（UFO）。这两个外流成分具有极端能量特征：质量外流率~20 M⊙/yr（各占吸积率的15%），动能功率分别达到辐射光度的~1%和~10%，是已知最强大的UFO之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究宇宙正午（z~2-3）时期高光度类星体的吸积与反馈机制，特别是超爱丁顿吸积类星体的极端物理特性，以及超快外流对星系演化的影响。

Method: 利用XMM-Newton和NuSTAR对WISSH13进行同步观测，获得最高质量的宽带X射线光谱（0.3-80 keV）。通过物理模型对连续谱进行建模分析，检测吸收特征并拟合超快外流的运动学和能量学参数。

Result: 1) 软光子指数Γ~2，强反射R~1.4-1.8，低高能截止E_cut~60-80 keV；2) 检测到两个吸收特征（~7.5 keV和~10 keV），置信度分别为96.7%和98.9%；3) 两个超快外流成分速度分别为~0.1c和~0.3c；4) 慢速成分在2017年观测中持续存在，快速成分仅在2024年检测到；5) 每个外流成分的质量外流率~20 M⊙/yr（占吸积率15%），动能功率分别达辐射光度的~1%和~10%。

Conclusion: WISSH13是已知最强大的超快外流之一，但相较于低红移AGN，其动能功率占辐射光度的比例相似。这表明超爱丁顿吸积类星体的外流反馈机制可能具有普适性。研究还揭示了外流的分层结构（两层）和时变特性，为理解宇宙正午时期类星体与其宿主星系的共同演化提供了重要观测约束。

Abstract: We present the first results from the WISSHFUL program, an XMM-Newton heritage program targeting luminous QSOs at Cosmic Noon. We report on recent simultaneous XMM-Newton and NuSTAR observations of the Super-Eddington accreting quasar WISSH13 at z=3.294, which provide the highest quality broadband X-ray spectrum to date for a non-lensed QSO at this redshift. Physical modeling of the continuum reveals a soft photon index ($Γ\sim2$) and strong reflection ($R\sim1.4-1.8$), despite the weak narrow Fe emission, and a low high-energy cut-off ($E_{cut}\sim60-80$ keV, $kTe = 15-20$ keV, depending on the model adopted). Most notably, we detect two significant (at $96.7\%$ and $98.9\%$ confidence level, respectively) absorption features at $\sim7.5$ and $\sim10$ keV rest-frame, interpreted as a blueshifted blend of Fe XXV He$α$ and Fe XXVI Ly$α$. These features indicate the presence of two kinematic components of a highly ionized, high column Ultra-Fast Outflow (UFO) with a velocity of $v_{out}\sim0.1c$ and $v_{out}\sim0.3c$, respectively. The slower wind is consistently detected in an archival 2017 XMM-Newton observation, whereas the faster wind is detected only in 2024. This stratified and variable wind exhibits extreme energetics, with a mass outflow rate of $\dot{M}_{out}\sim20M_{\odot}/yr$ (corresponding to $15\% \dot{M}_{acc}$) for each component, and a kinetic power of the order of $\sim1$ and $\sim10\%$ of the bolometric luminosity, respectively. While this represents one of the most powerful UFOs ever detected, its kinetic power is a similar fraction of the QSO's bolometric luminosity compared to lower-redshift AGN. We present several theoretical frameworks to explain the peculiar accretion and ejection properties of this remarkable QSO at Cosmic Noon.

</details>


### [120] [How Common Are Common Envelopes? Quantifying Their Role in Forming Gravitational-Wave Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05322)
*Floor S. Broekgaarden,Ana Lam,Sasha Levina,Jakub Klencki,Kyle A. Rocha,Lieke van Son,Steffani M. Grondin,Monica Gallegos-Garcia,Brian D. Metzger,Enrico Ramirez-Ruiz,Angela Twum,Melanie Santiago,Julia Haynes,Tyler B. Smith,Amedeo Romagnolo,Edo Berger,Lucas M. de Sá*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统比较200多个孤立双星星族合成模拟，发现BBH和BHNS的形成路径在是否经历共有包层（CE）阶段上覆盖0-100%的范围，但常预测相似并合率，存在简并性；而BNS形成几乎都涉及至少一次CE阶段（>90-100%）。并合率测量无法唯一约束形成路径，需依赖透明定义、可复现流程及多维度观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波天文学的核心目标之一是利用并合双星系统作为"化石"反演大质量恒星的形成与演化历史。然而，不同星族合成模型在主导轨道硬化机制（尤其是共有包层演化是否必需）上存在严重分歧，阻碍了可靠的天体物理推断。

Method: 汇编并系统比较超过200个孤立双星星族合成模拟，将其组织在统一的分层分类体系中，对比不同形成通道（有CE vs 无CE）对BBH、BHNS和BNS的贡献，并分析关键物理假设（质量转移稳定性、角动量损失、CE效率、超新星物理）的影响。

Result: BBH和BHNS的形成路径在CE主导到无CE主导的全范围（0-100%）内分布，但常预测相似的并合率，存在根本性的简并性；BNS形成则几乎全部依赖至少一次CE阶段（>90-100%）。关键物理参数非线性且关联性地影响通道贡献，单一参数变化趋势无法跨框架推广。

Conclusion: 仅靠并合率测量无法唯一约束形成路径。稳健解释引力波星族需要透明的形成通道定义、可复现的分析流程、系统性跨代码比较，以及超越并合率的多维度观测约束。

Abstract: A central goal of gravitational-wave astronomy is to use merging binary black hole (BBH), black hole-neutron star (BHNS), and binary neutron star (BNS) systems as fossils to reconstruct the formation and evolution of massive stars across cosmic time. In practice, this inference relies on population-synthesis models that map massive stellar binaries to merging compact objects. However, these models disagree on the dominant orbital-hardening mechanisms within isolated binary evolution, particularly on whether common-envelope (CE) evolution is required. To address this, we compile and systematically compare formation-channel predictions from more than 200 isolated-binary population-synthesis simulations, organized within a unified hierarchical taxonomy. We find that BBH and BHNS formation pathways span nearly the full allowed range from CE-dominated to without-CE-dominated evolution (0-100%), while often predicting similar merger rates, revealing a fundamental degeneracy: merger-rate measurements alone do not uniquely constrain the underlying evolutionary pathways. In contrast, BNS formation proceeds almost exclusively through channels involving at least one CE phase (>90-100%), suggesting CE evolution plays a qualitatively different role in BNS than in BBH and BHNS formation. The relative contributions of with-CE and without-CE pathways are governed primarily by assumptions controlling mass-transfer stability, angular-momentum loss, CE efficiency, and supernova physics, which often act non-linearly and in correlated fashion, such that trends from one-at-a-time parameter variations do not generalize across simulation frameworks. Robust interpretation of gravitational-wave populations will therefore require transparent formation-channel definitions, reproducible analysis pipelines, systematic cross-code comparisons, and observational constraints that extend beyond merger rates alone.

</details>


### [121] [Constraining Orbital Eccentricity of a Supermassive Black Hole Binary Candidate PKS 2131-0211](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05343)
*Avinash Kumar Paladi,A. Gopakumar,Sushmita Agarwal,Fazal Kareem*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文分析了类星体PKS 2131-021的射电光变曲线，发现其存在正弦调制。通过引入轨道偏心率改进运动学轨道模型，贝叶斯分析显示偏心率e=0.053±0.015（贝叶斯因子3.15），但考虑红噪声后（DRW过程）圆形轨道模型更优，偏心率为e<0.15。最终Circular+DRW模型被强烈支持，能稳定恢复出连贯的周期信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PKS 2131-021的射电光变曲线呈现正弦变化，前期研究（O'Neill et al. 2022）提出该现象源于双超大质量黑洞系统中相对论性多普勒增辉效应。然而原有模型假设双黑洞为圆轨道，未考虑轨道偏心率的影响，本文旨在将开普勒轨道参数解引入模型，检验偏心轨道能否更好地解释观测数据。

Method: (1) 将Keplerian参数解引入运动学轨道模型，描述双黑洞椭圆轨道；(2) 利用1975-2021年间Haystack、UMRAO、OVRO三个射电天文台的观测数据；(3) 采用贝叶斯参数估计方法约束轨道偏心率；(4) 引入Damped Random Walk（DRW）过程建模数据中的红噪声，对比圆形模型与偏心模型的优劣。

Result: (1) 不考虑红噪声时，偏心率e=0.053±0.015，贝叶斯因子3.15偏向偏心模型；(2) 考虑DRW红噪声后，圆形模型更优，偏心率上限e<0.15；(3) Circular+DRW模型被强烈支持，能稳定恢复各数据集中的连贯周期信号，轨道周期定义良好。

Conclusion: PKS 2131-021双黑洞系统的轨道偏心率极小，数据更支持圆轨道+红噪声模型。该研究为理解类星体周期性光变机制和双超大质量黑洞动力学提供了重要约束。

Abstract: A detailed analysis of the decades-long radio light curve of blazar PKS 2131-021 showed epochs of sinusoidal variations in the radio flux density time-series as detailed in O'Neill et al. (2022). The observed sinusoidal flux modulation arises naturally from relativistic Doppler boosting of the jet when the jet-emitting supermassive black hole (SMBH) orbits its companion. For SMBHs in circular orbits, this scenario yields sinusoidal light curves, offering a simple kinematic explanation for the observed variability in PKS 2131-021. We present an approach that incorporates the effects of orbital eccentricity into the Kinematic Orbital model for PKS 2131-021, using the Keplerian parametric solution to describe the SMBH binary orbit. Using the available radio light curve data, we demonstrate that the proposed SMBH binary likely possesses a residual orbital eccentricity, which we constrain through detailed Bayesian parameter estimation studies to be 0.053 \pm 0.015 with a Bayes factor of 3.15 over the circular model. However, when the analysis accounts for the presence of red noise in the data using a Damped Random Walk (DRW) process, the circular model is preferred, giving an eccentricity upper limit of e < 0.15. Nevertheless, our efforts reveal that the Circular+DRW model is strongly favored. This model consistently recovers a coherent periodic signal across all datasets, with the orbital period remaining well-defined even when accounting for broader uncertainties. This analysis incorporated archival observations from the Haystack Observatory, the University of Michigan Radio Astronomy Observatory (UMRAO), and the Owens Valley Radio Observatory (OVRO), spanning the period from 1975 to 2021, compiled by O'Neill et al. (2022).

</details>


### [122] [Complex Analysis of Askaryan Radiation: UHECR Reconstruction with Askaryan Radio Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05514)
*J. C. Hanson,D. Ibanez-Rodriguez*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个解析模型，证实南极阿斯卡良射电阵列（ARA）观测到的13个超高能宇宙射线（UHECR）候选事件确为UHECR。模型结合了阿斯卡良辐射和ARA射频通道响应，相干求和波形与模型的相关性系数在0.69-0.86之间，功率差异极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARA已观测到13个UHECR候选事件，需要从理论上确认这些事件确实来源于超高能宇宙射线，而非其他背景信号。

Method: 建立一个解析模型，包含阿斯卡良效应辐射和ARA射频通道响应，通过相干求和波形（CSWs）与模型进行匹配，计算相关性系数和分数功率差异来验证事件。

Result: 13个UHECR候选事件的相干求和波形与解析模型的相关性系数在0.69到0.86之间，且分数功率差异极小。

Conclusion: 该解析模型成功证实了ARA观测到的事件确为UHECR，并且可以利用该解析形式得到阿斯卡良电场，结果与ARA一致。

Abstract: Ultra-high energy cosmic rays (UHECR) can produce relativistic cascades that emit radio-frequency (RF) pulses in the 0.05-1 GHz bandwidth via two distinct effects: the geomagnetic effect, and the Askaryan effect. The geomagnetic effect occurs when the magnetic field of the Earth causes cascade charges to form a transverse current that radiates linearly polarized radiation aligned with the Lorentz force direction. The Askaryan effect is caused by the net negative charge excess in the cascade that radiates linearly polarized radiation along the Cherenkov cone. When UHECR cascades enter solid, RF transparent matter at altitudes where the cascade develops, Askaryan radiation can propagate through the solid matter to RF detectors. The Askaryan Radio Array (ARA) at the South Pole has observed 13 UHECR candidates in precisely this fashion. We present an analytical model that confirms the events are UHECRs. The model includes Askaryan radiation and the ARA RF channel response. The coherently summed waveforms (CSWs) from the UHECR candidates match our model with correlation coefficients between 0.69 and 0.86, and with minimal fractional power differences. Finally, we demonstrate how to use the analytic formalism to obtain the Askaryan $\vec{E}$-field, and that it matches the results from ARA.

</details>


### [123] [Joint Multi-Period Fermi-LAT and LHAASO Constraints on Axion-Like Particles from Mrk 421 Using Profile Likelihood with Gaussian Copula Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05738)
*Longhua Qin,Jiancheng Wang,Chuyuan Yang,Huaizhen Li,Ao Wang,Weiwei Na,Hushan Xu,Xiaogu Zhong,Zunli Yuan,Yubin Li,Guangbo Long*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文构建了多历元联合轮廓似然框架，使用高斯Copula（ρ=0.03）处理观测时段间的弱相关，通过Mrk 421的Fermi-LAT和LHAASO五组数据约束轴子粒子（ALPs），在低质量区间（≲1 neV）给出95%置信上限g_aγ≤8.33×10⁻¹⁴ GeV⁻¹，在0.1-500 neV区间给出保守界限g_aγ<6.50×10⁻¹² GeV⁻¹（双区）和7.30×10⁻¹² GeV⁻¹（单区）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统独立似然相加方法在处理多历元观测数据时忽略了不同观测时段间的统计相关性，导致参数估计不稳定和统计不一致，需要发展一种能够处理弱相关性的联合似然框架来更可靠地约束轴子粒子参数。

Method: 采用多历元联合轮廓似然方法，引入高斯Copula（ρ=0.03）建模不同观测期间的弱相关性；在无ALP零假设下全局优化 nuisance 参数后再对ALP质量m_a和耦合常数g_aγ进行轮廓化，以提升数值稳定性和统计一致性；使用Fermi-LAT和LHAASO对Mrk 421的五组同步观测数据，分别采用双区混合模型和单区强子喷流模型进行分析。

Result: 在CAST灵敏的低质量区间（m_a≲1 neV），95%置信水平上限g_aγ=8.33×10⁻¹⁴ GeV⁻¹；在0.1-500 neV宽质量范围内，双区模型给出g_aγ<6.50×10⁻¹² GeV⁻¹，单区模型给出g_aγ<7.30×10⁻¹² GeV⁻¹（均为保守界限）。

Conclusion: LHAASO的宽能段甚高能覆盖和Mrk 421的长期监测数据有效地改进了ALP约束；所开发的框架具有鲁棒性和可扩展性，适用于未来下一代伽马射线天文台的多信使ALP搜索。

Abstract: We construct a multi-epoch joint profile-likelihood framework to constrain axion-like particles (ALPs), employing a Gaussian copula ($ρ=0.03$) to account for weak correlations among distinct observational periods. Different from traditional independent likelihood addition, our scheme globally optimizes nuisance parameters under the no-ALP hypothesis prior to profiling ALP mass $m_a$ and coupling $g_{aγ}$, enhancing numerical stability and statistical consistency. Using five sets of simultaneous Fermi-LAT and LHAASO data of Mrk 421, we implement both two-zone hybrid and single-zone hadronic jet models. In the CAST-sensitive low-mass regime $m_a\lesssim1$ neV, the 95\% CL upper limit of $g_{aγ}$ is $8.33\times10^{-14}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$. Over $0.1$-$500$ neV, conservative bounds read $g_{aγ}<6.50\times10^{-12}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$ (two-zone) and $g_{aγ}<7.30\times10^{-12}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$ (single-zone). These results benefit from LHAASO's broadband very high energy coverage and long term monitoring of Mrk 421; the developed framework provides a robust and scalable pipeline suitable for future multi-messenger ALP searches with next-generation gamma-ray observatories.

</details>


### [124] [The X-ray emission of the long-period transient and accreting cataclysmic variable ASKAP J174508.9-505149](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05842)
*M. Imbrogno,M. Veresvarska,Y. L. Wang,N. Rea,F. Coti Zelati,K. Rose,J. Pritchard,D. de Martino,S. Scaringi,Z. Wang,D. L. Kaplan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对长周期暂现源ASKAP J174508.9-505149进行了详细的X射线变异性研究，确认其为吸积型激变变星，并测得其X射线周期为4868秒，与射电和光学周期一致。这是第三颗在X射线波段探测到的LPT，第二颗具有X射线周期性和可变X射线发射，第一颗被确认为吸积型磁性激变变星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期暂现源对现有致密天体射电周期性脉动机制构成挑战，而新发现的LPT ASKAP J174508.9-505149被归类为吸积型激变变星，但其X射线特性尚未被详细研究。

Method: 利用XMM-Newton和Einstein Probe在2025年9月至2026年5月间的观测数据，结合同步光学和射电观测，进行时变分析和能谱分析。

Result: 测得X射线周期P=4868(22)s，与射电和光学周期一致；硬度比在同一周期上变化，在调制最小值处达到峰值；存在长周期调制但受数据限制；能谱包含约0.1 keV的黑体成分、约15 keV的碰撞电离等离子体以及0.77 keV的吸收特征（可能为Oxygen-VII）。

Conclusion: ASKAP J174508.9-505149是第三颗在X射线波段探测到的LPT，第二颗具有X射线周期性和可变X射线发射的LPT，第一颗被确认为吸积型磁性激变变星的LPT。

Abstract: Long-period transients (LPTs) challenge our knowledge of the mechanism producing radio periodic pulsations in compact objects. Some LPTs have been associated with systems hosting a white dwarf and a low-mass star in a detached binary. Recently, a new LPT (ASKAP\,J174508.9-505149) has been classified as an accreting cataclysmic variable (CV). In the present letter, we report on the detailed study of the X-ray variability of ASKAP\,J174508.9-505149 as observed by \emph{XMM-Newton} and \emph{Einstein Probe} between September 2025 and May 2026. Simultaneous optical and radio observations are also presented. We studied the timing variability of the source, and estimated an X-ray periodicity of $P=4868(22)$\,s, consistent with radio and optical periods. We also observe the same periodicity in the hardness ratio extracted from the \emph{XMM-Newton} observation, peaking at the minimum of the modulation. A long-term modulation is also present in the X-rays and in the B-band photometry, but it is poorly constrained by the current dataset. Spectral X-ray analysis shows the presence of a black-body component ($\sim$0.1\,keV), a collisionally ionised plasma ($\sim$15\,keV), and an absorption feature at 0.77 keV (possibly due to Oxygen-VII). This is the third LPT detected in the X-ray band, the second with a detected X-ray periodicity and variable X-ray emission, and the first conclusively recognised as an accreting magnetic CV.

</details>


### [125] [Modeling Gamma-Ray Burst Spectra with Convolutional Neural Networks: Fast-Cooling Synchrotron Emission in a Decaying Magnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05910)
*Jia-Ming Chen,Ke-Rui Zhu,Shan Chang,Zhao-Yang Peng,Yong-Gang Zheng,Li Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于卷积神经网络（CNN）的谱仿真器，用于模拟伽马射线暴（GRB）瞬时辐射中衰减磁场的快冷却同步辐射模型，将该模型计算速度提升至毫秒级，并将其纳入贝叶斯谱分析框架应用于GRB 231020A的Fermi/GBM数据，结果表明衰减磁场模型比标准快冷却模型能更好地拟合观测数据，为GRB瞬时辐射机制提供了更物理的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRB瞬时辐射的辐射机制仍不确定，虽然衰减磁场中的快冷却同步辐射模型可以解释非热谱形状，但其计算成本过高，限制了系统性的观测拟合和统计模型比较。

Method: 开发基于CNN的谱仿真器，在物理驱动的参数空间上生成大规模合成数据集进行训练；将训练好的仿真器嵌入贝叶斯谱分析框架中。

Result: 训练后的网络以高保真度复现数值谱，计算成本降至毫秒级；在GRB 231020A的大部分时间区间内，衰减磁场快冷却同步辐射模型比标准模型具有更好的拟合质量和更小的贝叶斯信息准则值。

Conclusion: 径向衰减磁场为该暴的瞬时辐射谱提供了更合理、更具物理动机的解释；该仿真器为大样本贝叶斯推断和GRB瞬时辐射模型的系统比较提供了实用途径。

Abstract: The radiation mechanism of gamma-ray burst (GRB) prompt emission remains uncertain. Although the fast-cooling synchrotron model in a decaying magnetic field can account for the characteristic nonthermal spectral shape, its computational cost has limited its use in systematic observational fitting and statistical model comparison. We develop a convolutional neural network (CNN)-based spectral emulator for this physical model and train it on a large synthetic data set generated over a physically motivated parameter space. The trained network reproduces the numerical spectra with high fidelity while reducing the cost of spectral evaluation to the millisecond level. We then incorporate the emulator into a Bayesian spectral-analysis framework and apply it to the time-resolved spectra of GRB 231020A observed by Fermi/GBM. In most time intervals, the decaying-field fast-cooling synchrotron model provides better fits and smaller Bayesian information criterion values than the standard fast-cooling synchrotron model. These results suggest that a radially decaying magnetic field provides a plausible and more physically motivated interpretation of the prompt-emission spectrum of this burst, while also indicating that the emulator offers a practical route for large-sample Bayesian inference and systematic comparisons of GRB prompt-emission models.

</details>


### [126] [Simulations of interaction between outflow and surrounding broken power-law circumnuclear medium: implications for different radio light curves of TDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05944)
*Xiangli Lei,Qingwen Wu,Chang Zhou,Wei-Hua Lei,Ya-Ping Li,Jiancheng Wu,Weibo Yang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过三维流体动力学模拟，系统研究了潮汐瓦解事件（TDE）中 outflow 与 circumnuclear medium（CNM）在不同参数空间下的相互作用，揭示了双峰射电光变曲线的物理起源，并解释了不同类型TDE射电观测现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TDE的复杂射电光变曲线挑战了我们对超质量黑洞周围外流和CNM性质的理解，需要系统性研究outflow-CNM相互作用机制。

Method: 采用三维流体动力学模拟，使用在Bondi半径附近存在过渡的分段幂律CNM密度轮廓，在广阔参数空间内探索outflow-CNM相互作用。

Result: Bondi半径内的outflow-CNM相互作用产生早期射电耀斑（≤2年）；若outflow在Bondi半径外减速，数年后会出现第二次射电再增亮。高密度内部CNM或稀薄外部CNM抑制晚期再增亮产生单早期峰值耀斑；稀薄内部CNM抑制早期耀斑产生单晚期峰值事件；高密度外部CNM触发急剧的晚期上升。相对论性喷流与分段幂律CNM相互作用可复现喷流TDE的特征光变曲线。

Conclusion: CNM密度轮廓的破碎幂律结构（Bondi半径附近过渡）是解释TDE多样射电光变曲线的关键因素，无需引入复杂的喷流结构。

Abstract: The complex radio light curves of tidal disruption events (TDEs) challenge our understanding of the properties of both the outflows and the circumnuclear medium (CNM) surrounding supermassive black holes. In this work, we explore outflow-CNM interactions across a broad parameter space using three-dimensional hydrodynamic simulations, adopting a broken power-law CNM density profile with a transition near the Bondi radius. The outflow-CNM interaction inside Bondi radius produces an early radio flare (\(\lesssim 2\) yr) once the emitting region becomes optically thin. A second radio rebrightening can appear a few years later if the outflow decelerates beyond Bondi radius. We also find that either a very dense inner CNM, which causes rapid deceleration, or a rarefied outer CNM suppresses the late rebrightening that will produces a single early-peaked flare. In contrast, a rarefied CNM inside the Bondi radius suppresses the early flare and yields a single late-peaked event. For the case of very dense CNM at large radii, the interaction will trigger a sharp late-time rise as observed in some TDEs. We further explore the interaction of a relativistic jet with a broken power-law CNM, which can reproduce the characteristic light curves as observed in jetted TDEs without invoking complex jet structure.

</details>


### [127] [Detectability of secondary images from flares near Sgr A* with mock GRAVITY data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06134)
*Fengting Xie,Qing-Hua Zhu,Xin Li*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究通过模拟GRAVITY未来观测数据，探讨了从Sgr A*耀斑轨道运动中探测次级像的可行性。研究发现，仅增加样本量无法可靠区分次级像信号，需同时将样本量提升一个数量级并将天体测量不确定度降至当前GRAVITY数据的40%，才能实现稳健检测（|ΔBIC|>7.9）。次级像的探测是迈向研究高阶像和光子环的关键第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRAVITY合作组报道的近红外耀斑轨道运动编码了吸积物质动力学和时空几何信息，而耀斑的质心轨迹包含主像、次级像及高阶像的贡献，可能揭示时空几何的独特特征。本研究旨在探索这些未被分辨的次级像是否可被观测检测到。

Method: 通过模拟未来GRAVITY观测的mock数据，比较两种模型：质心仅由主像轨迹决定，与质心包含主像和次级像的流量加权贡献。基于贝叶斯框架拟合模型，量化次级像信号可被统计区分的条件。

Result: 仅将样本量提升一个数量级不足以提供区分次级像的强证据。稳健检测需要同时将样本量提升一个数量级，并将天体测量不确定度降低至当前GRAVITY天体测量数据的40%（|ΔBIC|>7.9）。

Conclusion: 与主导吸积流物理的主像不同，次级像源于强场引力透镜效应。探测次级像是迈向研究高阶像及光子环的必要第一步。

Abstract: The orbital motion of near-infrared flares reported by the GRAVITY collaboration encodes information about both the dynamics of accretion matter and the underlying spacetime geometry. The centroid track of these flares, which corresponds to the flux-weighted center of light, incorporates contributions from primary, secondary and higher-order images. Thus, it potentially indicates distinctive signatures of the spacetime geometry, even when these individual multiple images remain unresolved. In this study, we explore the detectability of the secondary images from flares orbiting Sgr A* through mock data simulating future GRAVITY observations. Specifically, we compare the model in which the centroid coincides with the track of the primary images with another model in which the centroid incorporates flux-weighted contributions from both the primary and secondary images. Fitting these models to the mock data based on Bayesian framework, we quantify the conditions under which the signature of secondary images can be statistically distinguishable. We demonstrate that increasing the sample size by an order of magnitude alone could not yield strong evidence for distinguishing the secondary image. Robust detectability ($|Δ\text{BIC}| >7.9$) is achieved when both with the improved sample size and astrometric uncertainties reduced to 40\% of current uncertainties of GRAVITY astrometric data. Unlike the primary image, which is dominated by accretion flow physics, the secondary images originate from gravitational lensing in the strong-field regime. Their detection is an essential first step toward probing higher-order images and the photon rings.

</details>


### [128] [Probing a new subclass of llGRB-SN transients: Insights from EP250304a and its associated supernova](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06213)
*L. Cotter,A. Martin-Carrillo,R. A. J. Eyles-Ferris,L. Izzo,D. B. Malesani,Y. Julakanti,G. Corcoran,A. Saccardi,P. G. Jonker,A. J. Levan,F. Carotenuto,P. T. O'Brien,J. H. Gillanders,J. N. Sanchez-Sierras,J. Sollerman,A. Andersson,A. Aryan,T. de Boer,J. S. Bright,K. C. Chambers,M. Gromadzki,M. E. Huber,C. Inserra,T. Lowe,P. Minguez,G. S. Narayan,M. Nicholl,G. S. H. Paek,A. Sedgewick,K. W. Smith,J. W. Tweddle,S. Yang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: EP250304a/SN 2025fhm是一例由爱因斯坦探针（EP）发现、与Ic型宽谱超新星成协的低光度伽马射线暴（llGRB-SN）事件。其多波段观测显示存在相对论性喷流，但未探测到伽马射线发射。通过细致的光谱和测光分析及光变曲线建模，发现该事件与GRB 060218等已知llGRB-SNe高度相似，表现为热激波茧主导的早期辐射，可能代表了一类新的激波茧主导llGRB-SNe子类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EP开启了探测以往无法发现的微弱低光度暂现源的新时代，EP250304a作为EP发现的事件，其缺乏伽马射线发射但存在相对论性喷流证据的特殊性，促使研究者深入探究其物理本质与分类归属。

Method: 对EP250304a/SN 2025fhm进行详细的光谱和测光分析，利用Redback Python软件包开展多波段光变曲线建模，并与已知llGRB-SNe事件进行系统比较。

Result: EP250304a/SN 2025fhm与GRB 060218/SN 2006aj、GRB 100316D/SN 2010bh和GRB 171205A/SN 2017iuk等llGRB-SNe高度相似，均表现出热激波茧主导的早期发射特征。

Conclusion: EP250304a/SN 2025fhm可能属于一个新兴的激波茧主导llGRB-SNe子类，代表了引擎驱动的GRB-SN爆发连续谱中的低光度极端。

Abstract: With the advent of the Einstein Probe (EP) mission, we are entering a new era in the study of gamma-ray bursts (GRBs), enabling the detection of faint, low-luminosity transients that would previously have gone undetected. EP250304a was an event discovered by EP associated with the broad-lined type Ic supernova (SN) SN 2025fhm located at z = 0.2. Despite no gamma-ray emission being detected at the time of the EP trigger, we identify evidence for a relativistic outflow consistent with a GRB-like jet across multiple wavelengths. We present a detailed spectral and photometric analysis of EP250304a/SN 2025fhm, including multi-band light curve modelling performed with the Redback Python package. We find that this event closely resembles low-luminosity GRB-SNe (llGRB-SNe) such as GRB 060218/SN 2006aj, GRB 100316D/SN 2010bh, and GRB 171205A/SN 2017iuk, all of which exhibit early-time emission consistent with a thermal shocked cocoon. These similarities suggest that EP250304A/SN 2025fhm may belong to an emerging subclass of shocked cocoon-dominated llGRB-SNe, representing the low-luminosity end of a broader continuum of engine-driven GRB-SN explosions.

</details>


### [129] [A micronova burst in the intermediate polar IGR J17014-4306](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06305)
*Alexandre S. Oliveira,D. C. Souza,G. J. M. Luna,C. V. Rodrigues*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 在TESS数据中探测到食双星系统IGR J17014-4306的一次光学短爆发，其参数与微新星爆发一致，这是目前已知轨道周期最长的中介偏振星中发现的微新星事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已知最长轨道周期的食双星中介偏振星IGR J17014-4306中寻找并表征短时标光学爆发事件，以验证微新星现象在此类系统中的存在。

Method: 利用TESS数据检测光学爆发，结合Gaia、ASAS-SN和AAVSO长期光变曲线搜索类似事件；通过时序分析研究爆发前后白矮星自旋周期的稳定性。

Result: 探测到持续1.56天、峰值光度约9.3×10^33 erg/s、总能量约3.25×10^38 erg的光学爆发；在~11年的数据中找到16个可能的快速增亮事件；爆发期间功率谱变复杂出现多峰，但自旋周期保持稳定。

Conclusion: IGR J17014-4306被确认为微新星系统（总数增至8个），其极端轨道周期和食双星特性使其成为研究磁约束白矮星热核燃烧的理想测试平台。

Abstract: We report the detection of a short optical burst in TESS data of IGR J17014-4306, the eclipsing intermediate polar with the longest known orbital period. The burst lasts 1.56 d and shows multiple peaks, reaching $(9.3 \pm 0.2) \times 10^{33}$ erg s$^{-1}$, and releases a total energy of $(3.25 \pm 0.01) \times 10^{38}$ erg. The burst parameters are consistent with those of a micronova eruption, currently understood as a thermonuclear runaway in the magnetically-confined accretion column. From its energy, we infer a burned column mass of $\sim 1.6 \times 10^{-11}$ M$_\odot$, which implies a recurrence time of $\sim 20$ d. Our search for similar events in long-term Gaia, ASAS-SN, and AAVSO light curves reveals 16 possible fast brightenings over $\sim 11$ yr, suggesting that micronova events may be frequent in IGR J17014-4306. Timing analysis of the TESS data shows that the white-dwarf spin period remains stable before and after the burst. During the burst, however, the power spectrum becomes more complex and exhibits multiple peaks. The classification of IGR J17014-4306 as a micronova brings the total number of confirmed systems to eight. Its extreme orbital period and eclipsing nature make it an ideal test-bed for further studies of magnetically confined thermonuclear burning on white dwarfs.

</details>


### [130] [A high-energy neutrino flare associated with nearby bright interacting supernova SN 2021foa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06409)
*Ming-Xuan Lu,Yun-Feng Liang,Xue-Rui Ouyang,Da-Bin Lin,Xiang-Gao Wang,Yi-Zhong Fan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报告了 IceCube 在相互作用超新星 SN 2021foa 方向探测到高能中微子 excess 的证据，该中微子簇射在时间上与 SN 2021foa 的光学峰值重合（发现后约16-22天），统计显著性约4.0σ。SN 2021foa 是一种独特的"翻转"超新星，其光谱在50天内于 IIn 和 Ibn 型之间反复转换，表明前身星经历了剧烈复杂的质量损失历史。中微子能量远超超新星的光学辐射能和抛射动能，表明中微子辐射可能由延迟的中心引擎驱动的、被稠密星周介质扼流的喷流所供能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心坍缩超新星被认为是潜在的中微子源，但一直缺乏确凿的观测证据。本文旨在通过 IceCube 数据寻找超新星的高能中微子发射证据，以验证超新星作为高能中微子源的可行性。

Method: 利用 IceCube 中微子观测站的第二期缪子径迹数据，对 SN 2021foa 方向进行时间依赖分析，通过最大检验统计量（~28.2）识别中微子簇射，并利用蒙特卡洛模拟评估显著性。

Result: 探测到与 SN 2021foa 光学峰值时间重合的中微子 excess，随机概率约为 6.7×10⁻⁵，对应显著性约 4.0σ。中微子能量远超超新星光学辐射能和抛射动能，表明可能存在延迟中心引擎驱动的被扼流喷流。

Conclusion: SN 2021foa 是高能中微子与超新星之间存在时空关联的强有力证据，其高能中微子发射可能源于中心引擎驱动的喷流与稠密星周介质的相互作用，这为理解超新星作为宇宙线和高能中微子源提供了新的视角。

Abstract: While core-collapse supernovae have been widely discussed as potential neutrino sources, definitive observational evidence has remained elusive. In this work, we report evidence of high-energy neutrino emission in the direction of supernova SN 2021foa, which is one of the closest and brightest interacting supernovae observed to date. Using the second data release of muon track data from the IceCube Neutrino Observatory, we conducted a time-dependent analysis and identified a neutrino clustering that temporally coincides with the optical peak of SN 2021foa, occurring approximately 16 to 22 days after the discovery date, with a maximum test statistic of $\sim 28.2$. Monte Carlo simulations indicate that the probability of observing such a neutrino excess by chance is $\sim6.7 \times 10^{-5}$, corresponding to a significance of $\sim4.0\,σ$. The spatial and temporal correlation strongly suggests that the neutrinos originate from the supernova. SN 2021foa is a unique "flip-flop" supernova; its spectra repeatedly transitioned between hydrogen-rich (Type IIn) and helium-rich (Type Ibn) phases within 50 days post-peak, reflecting a violent and complex mass-loss history of its progenitor. The inferred neutrino energy exceeds the optical radiative energy and ejecta kinetic energy of the supernova by orders of magnitude, suggesting that the neutrino emission is likely powered by a delayed central engine driving a jet that is choked within the dense circumstellar medium.

</details>


### [131] [Analytic insight into the physics of SASI II. Spiral instability of the prograde mode in a rotating stellar core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06471)
*T. Foglizzo*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文建立了旋转大质量恒星核坍缩过程中SASI主导本征频率的解析近似，揭示了差分旋转通过相位混合机制使顺行SASI模式进一步失稳的物理机制，并给出了10%精度的单臂与双臂螺旋模式解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解旋转大质量恒星核坍缩中SASI的非轴对称运动如何在中微子和引力波信号中产生特征频率印记，并阐明差分旋转对SASI模式稳定性的影响机制。

Method: 采用微扰分析计算球几何中停滞吸积激波的本征频率，将微扰方程表述为自驱振子形式以纳入差分旋转影响；利用相位混合理论解释差分旋转对顺行模式的作用；采用稳相近似分析含共转半径的强旋转情形。

Result: 获得单臂和双臂螺旋模式的本征频率解析表达式，精度约10%；发现差分旋转通过增加顺行模式涡度扰动的径向波长改善与声学结构的大尺度相位匹配，从而增强失稳；弱旋转下(~1/r²)相位混合效应在小半径处显著；强旋转下稳相近似揭示主导耦合位于共转区与激波之间。

Conclusion: SASI主导模式的振荡频率在中微子发射具体形式的依赖较弱（当激波半径超过最大减速半径的1.5倍时）；差分旋转即使不产生共转半径也能通过相位混合效应显著影响SASI稳定性。

Abstract: During the core-collapse of a rotating massive star, the standing accretion shock instability (SASI) favours the development of non-axisymmetric motions which can imprint specific frequency signatures on the neutrino and gravitational wave signals.This study establishes analytical approximations for the eigenfrequencies of the dominant SASI modes. It also explains the physical mechanism responsible for the further destabilization of prograde SASI modes by differential rotation. A perturbative analysis is used to calculate the eigenfrequencies of a stalled accretion shock in spherical geometry, taking into account the rotation of the collapsing stellar core. The formulation of the perturbative equations as a self-forced oscillator is extended to include differential rotation and interpret the results physically. The oscillation frequency of the dominant mode weakly depends on the detailed formulation of neutrino emission if the shock radius exceeds ~1.5 times the radius r_nabla of maximum deceleration. Analytical expressions are obtained for the one and two-armed spiral modes with a 10% accuracy in this regime. The effect of differential rotation is explained by the role of phase mixing between the advective forcing and the acoustic structure. The radial wavelength of vorticity perturbations associated with the prograde mode is increased by differential rotation, leading to a better phase match with the large radial scale of the acoustic structure. Even when rotation is too modest to involve a corotation radius, its adverse effect on phase mixing can be significant at small radius due to the steep inward increase of the rotation frequency ~1/r^2 in the region of stationary accretion. In the regime of stronger rotation involving a corotation radius, the stationary phase approximation sheds light on the dominant advective-acoustic coupling, located between the corotation zone and the shock.

</details>

### [132] [Eigenvector Spatial Filters Nuclear Norm Matrix Completion with Application to Air Quality Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05450)
*Rodolfo Metulini*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出ESFNNMC方法，通过引入Moran型特征向量捕捉空间自相关，改进了核范数矩阵补全在环境面板数据缺失值重建中的表现，在保持计算成本的同时显著提升了插补精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境面板数据中的缺失观测重建对暴露评估和政策分析至关重要，传统核范数矩阵补全方法忽略了空气质量过程中固有的空间依赖性。

Method: 提出ESFNNMC方法，用Moran型特征向量替代单位特定截距项以捕捉空间自相关；采用Block-Coordinate Descent算法求解多凸优化问题，结合软阈值奇异值分解和交叉验证正则化。

Result: 在多种缺失模式、时空自相关水平、矩阵维度及秩结构模拟下，ESFNNMC相比标准固定效应方法显著提升插补精度，且计算成本基本不变。应用于2021年意大利伦巴第64个监测站PM10数据。

Conclusion: ESFNNMC是一种有效且计算高效的环境面板数据缺失值插补方法，能充分利用空间自相关结构提升重建精度。

Abstract: Reliable reconstruction of missing observations in environmental panel datasets is essential for accurate exposure assessment and policy analysis. Traditional nuclear norm matrix completion methods effectively impute missing entries in low-rank matrices, yet often overlook the spatial dependence inherent to air quality processes. This paper introduces the Eigenvector Spatial Filters Nuclear Norm Matrix Completion (ESFNNMC) method, an extension of nuclear norm fixed-effects matrix completion that replaces unit-specific intercepts with a set of Moran-type eigenvectors capturing spatial autocorrelation in the data. To estimate the model, we propose a Block-Coordinate Descent (BCD) approach for multiconvex optimization problems, with soft-thresholded singular value decomposition and cross-validated regularization. Through comprehensive simulations varying missingness patterns, the level of spatial and temporal autocorrelation, and dimension, shape, and rank structure of the matrices, ESFNNMC demonstrates substantial improvements in imputation accuracy over the standard fixed-effects approach, while keeping the computational cost approximately unchanged. The method is applied to impute missing entries in daily PM10 measurements in 64 monitoring stations in Lombardy, Italy, during the year 2021.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

### [133] [Arithmetic Wu Formulas and the Generalized Hecke Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06008)
*Shachar Carmeli,Mark Shusterman,Sa'ar Zehavi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在S-整数环（2可逆）上有限分离型方案的Geisser-Schmidt/Milne修正紧支平展上同调上构造了典范的Steenrod平方运算，将Feng的绝对平展Wu类从有限域推广到算术基；证明了绝对Wu公式，并由此获得基上正则投射平坦方案的Chern类通用模2同余无限族（广义Hecke定理），推广了Hecke定理、Serre旋量丛Riemann-Hurwitz定理、Atiyah有限域theta特征定理及Shusterman-Sawin定理的3流形分支覆盖类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Feng在有限域上定义的绝对平展Wu类推广到更一般的算术基（S-整数环，2可逆），从而建立算术背景下Chern类的模2同余理论。

Method: 在修正紧支平展上同调上构造Steenrod平方操作；证明修正紧支相对Wu公式（推广Benoist相对Wu公式到算术紧支情形）；利用这些工具证明绝对Wu公式。

Result: 得到了正则投射平坦方案在有限域（奇特征）和S-整数环（2可逆）上的绝对Wu公式；获得了由算术形变的Hirzebruch 2-Todd级数控制的Chern类通用模2同余无限族（广义Hecke定理）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了算术基上广义Hecke定理，统一并推广了多个经典结果（Hecke定理、Serre定理、Atiyah定理、Shusterman-Sawin定理的3流形类比），为算术代数几何中的模2现象提供了统一的同伦论框架。

Abstract: We construct canonical Steenrod square operations on the Geisser--Schmidt/Milne modified compactly supported étale cohomology of separated finite-type schemes over rings of $S$-integers in which $2$ is invertible. This lets us extend Feng's notion of the absolute étale Wu class from the finite-field setting to arithmetic bases away from $2$. A key technical input is a modified compactly supported relative Wu formula, extending Benoist's relative Wu formula to the arithmetic compact-support setting. Using this, we prove an absolute Wu formula for regular projective flat schemes over either finite fields of odd characteristic or rings of $S$-integers away from $2$: if $f\colon X\to B$ is such a scheme, then the absolute Wu class of $X$ is the product of the relative Wu class $\operatorname{Sq}^{-1}(w_{\mathrm{et}}(τ_f))$ and the pullback of the absolute Wu class of the base. In the $S$-integer case, the base contribution is $1+β_B$, where $β_B$ is the Bockstein, equivalently the Kummer class of $-1$. As an application, we obtain an infinite family of universal mod-$2$ congruences among the Chern classes of regular projective flat schemes over such bases, governed by an arithmetic deformation of Hirzebruch's $2$-Todd series; this is the generalized Hecke theorem. In low dimensions these congruences recover Hecke's theorem on the different away from $2$, Serre's Riemann--Hurwitz theorem for spin bundles, Atiyah's theorem on theta characteristics over finite fields, and the smooth $3$-manifold branched-cover analogue of the Shusterman--Sawin theorem, while yielding new higher-dimensional congruences over both finite and arithmetic bases.

</details>


### [134] [Corrigendum to "Isomorphism classes of Drinfeld modules over finite fields"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05223)
*Valentijn Karemaker,Jeffrey Katen,Mihran Papikian*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对arXiv:2209.15033中定理5.4进行修正，指出原论文的主要定理A和B本身正确，但定理B的证明需用本文的新定理替换错误的定理5.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正arXiv:2209.15033中定理5.4的错误，确保定理B的证明正确性。

Method: 提供修正后的定理替换原论文中错误的定理5.4，以修正定理B的证明过程。

Result: 原论文的主要定理A和B仍然有效，但定理B的证明需要依据本文提供的修正定理进行修改。

Conclusion: 通过本注记的修正，原论文中的主要结论得以保留并得到正确证明支撑。

Abstract: In this note we provide corrections to Theorem 5.4 of the paper ``Isomorphism classes of Drinfeld modules over finite fields'', arXiv:2209.15033. The main theorems of this paper, Theorem A and B in its introduction, are valid as stated; in the proof of Theorem B the argument needs to be modified by replacing the erroneous Theorem 5.4 by the theorem of this note.

</details>


### [135] [Theorem $(1+1.9)$ on the Goldbach Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05224)
*Jiamin Li,Jianya Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过构造新的加权筛法和分析工具，无条件证明了命题(1+1.9)和(1-1.75)，并在Elliott-Halberstam猜想下改进到(1+1.4)和(1-1.4)，在Goldbach猜想和孪生素数猜想的逼近问题上取得了突破性进展，弥合了(1+2)与(1+1)以及(1-2)与(1-1)之间长期存在的理论鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，Goldbach猜想和孪生素数猜想的逼近研究中，命题(1+2)（Chen定理）与(1+1)（二元Goldbach猜想）之间，以及命题(1-2)与(1-1)（孪生素数猜想）之间存在显著的理论差距，作者旨在弥合这一鸿沟，推动相关研究取得突破。

Method: 构造新的加权筛法，并采用新的分析工具。

Result: 无条件证明命题(1+1.9)和(1-1.75)成立；在Elliott-Halberstam猜想下，将指数改进至1.4，即证明命题(1+1.4)和(1-1.4)。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一条连接(1+2)与(1+1)以及(1-2)与(1-1)的途径，在该研究方向上取得了突破性进展。

Abstract: For $1 \leq a \leq 2$, we say Proposition $(1+a)$ holds if every sufficiently large even integer $N$ can be written as $$N = p + rq, \quad r \leq q^{a-1},$$ where $r$ is either $1$ or prime, and $p,q$ are primes. Thus Proposition $(1+1)$ is essentially the binary Goldbach Conjecture, and Proposition $(1+2)$ is Chen's theorem. We prove unconditionally that Proposition $(1+1.9)$ is true. Assuming the Elliott--Halberstam Conjecture, the exponent $1.9$ can be improved to $1.4$. Analogously, Proposition $(1-a)$ is formulated for the Twin Prime Conjecture. Unconditionally, we prove Proposition $(1-1.75)$, and under the Elliott--Halberstam Conjecture, Proposition $(1-1.4)$. For six decades, a substantial theoretical divide has persisted between Propositions $(1+2)$ and $(1+1)$, and likewise between Propositions $(1-2)$ and $(1-1)$. By constructing new weighted sieves and adopting new analytic tools, this paper establishes a connecting pathway between them and achieves breakthroughs in this line of research.

</details>


### [136] [The Case of the Second Smallest Modulus: or why we cannot be arbitrarily careless?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05251)
*Katalin Gyarmati*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过一个极值论证，给出了一个简洁初等的证明，证明覆盖系统中第二小模数必须是有界的，从而简化了已知结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 覆盖系统第二小模数的有界性是一个重要的数论问题，已有的证明较为复杂，作者希望给出一个更简洁、初等的证明来简化已知结果。

Method: 采用极值论证（maximality argument）的方法，通过简洁初等的步骤推导出第二小模数的有界性。

Result: 成功证明了覆盖系统中第二小模数必须是有界的。

Conclusion: 该工作为覆盖系统的模数有界性问题提供了一个更简洁的证明路径，简化了此前较为复杂的推导过程。

Abstract: We present a concise, elementary proof that the second smallest modulus of a covering system must be bounded, simplifying the known results through a maximality argument.

</details>


### [137] [Hasse-Weil Zeta Functions Modulo a Prime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05340)
*Chris Hall*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上特征p≠r的r阶伽罗瓦覆盖的zeta函数模r约化，给出了其与底空间zeta函数及分歧轨迹的关系，并应用于曲线及超椭圆/超椭圆曲线的分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限域上伽罗瓦覆盖的zeta函数在模r下的约化行为，建立分歧轨迹与zeta函数约化之间的定量联系。

Method: 利用伽罗瓦群为素数阶r（r≠p）的有限态射π: Y→X，通过分歧轨迹Z⊂X来描述Y的zeta函数模r约化，并将其表示为X的zeta函数与Z相关因子的组合。

Result: 给出了Y的zeta函数模r约化的显式表达式，该表达式完全由X的zeta函数和分歧轨迹Z决定。

Conclusion: 该结果可应用于曲线情形以及超椭圆/超椭圆曲线分子（numerators）的模r约化计算。

Abstract: Let $\mathbb{F}_q$ be a finite field of characteristic $p$ and $π\colon Y\to X$ be a finite $\mathbb{F}_q$-morphism of separated $\mathbb{F}_q$-schemes of finite type. Suppose $π$ is generically Galois with group $G$ of prime order $r\neq p$. We determine the mod-$r$ reduction of the zeta function of $Y$ in terms of the zeta function of $X$ and the branch locus $Z\subset X$ of $π$. We give applications to curves and to numerators of hyperelliptic/superelliptic curves.

</details>


### [138] [On explicit Fourier expansions of theta lifts to ${\rm SO}(3,n+1)$ arising from elliptic newforms of level one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05549)
*Henry H. Kim,Takuya Yamauchi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过退化Whittaker函数和Eisenstein级数的显式计算，得到了从椭圆新形式到特殊正交群SO(3,n+1)的θ提升的Fourier展开的显式公式。这些θ提升是Hecke本征、非尖点、平方可积的自守形式，权重为l（l≥n+2，偶数），来源于SL₂(ℤ)上椭圆新形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究θ提升到特殊正交群SO(3,n+1)上的Fourier展开结构，以获得其显式表达。

Method: 利用退化Whittaker函数和对Eisenstein级数进行显式计算，推导出θ提升的Fourier展开公式。

Result: 得到了Hecke本征、非尖点、平方可积自守形式（权重l）的Fourier展开显式公式，这些形式来源于SL₂(ℤ)上特定权重的椭圆新形式。

Conclusion: 成功获得了θ提升到SO(3,n+1)上的Fourier展开的闭合形式表达式，建立了与椭圆新形式之间的显式联系。

Abstract: Using degenerate Whittaker functions and explicit computations of Eisenstein series, we obtain explicit formulas for the Fourier expansions of theta lifts to the special orthogonal group $G={\rm SO}(3,n+1)$ over $\mathbb{Q}$, where $n\ge 3$ and $G$ splits at all finite places. The theta lifts in question are Hecke eigen, non-cuspidal, square-integrable automorphic forms of weight $l$ ($l\ge n+2$, even), arising from elliptic newforms for $\SL_2(\Z)$ of weight $l-\frac{n-2}{2}$ when $n$ is even and $2l-n+1$ when $n$ is odd.

</details>


### [139] [Exotic and inverted Kloosterman sums over semisimple algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05771)
*Daqing Wan,Dingxin Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文引入了非交换有限维半单代数上的奇异Kloosterman和及其逆和，证明了它们可约化为交换etale代数上的对应和，并给出了平方根估计（逆和中可能出现显式修正项）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Kloosterman和理论推广到非交换半单代数框架下，建立更广泛的指数和理论。

Method: 通过构造非交换半单代数上的奇异Kloosterman和，证明其约化到交换etale代数上的公式，并应用代数数论和表示论工具推导估计。

Result: 成功约化到交换情形；获得平方根估计；逆和中出现显式修正项。

Conclusion: 建立了非交换半单代数上Kloosterman和的理论基础，其平方根估计暗示了类似Weil界的深层算术性质。

Abstract: We introduce exotic Kloosterman sums and exotic inverted Kloosterman sums attached to non-commutative finite-dimensional semisimple algebras over a finite field $\mathbf{F}_q$, and prove their reduction formulae to exotic Kloosterman and exotic inverted Kloosterman sums over commutative étale $\mathbf{F}_q$ algebras. We then obtain square-root estimates for these sums; for inverted sums an explicit correction term may appear.

</details>


### [140] [Drinfeld modules in rank 2 with CM and S-unit j-invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05820)
*Liam Baker,Fabien Pazuki,Patricio Perez Pina*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在F_q[T]上秩2的Drinfeld模的j-不变量的有限性结果（这些模具有CM性质且是S-单位），基于ordinary约化与超奇异约化理论以及分裂行为分析，给出了计算给定阶的CM j-不变量的算法，并提供了反例反驳Dorman猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Drinfeld模的CM j-不变量在S-单位条件下的有限性，并验证/反驳相关猜想（如Dorman猜想）。

Method: 通过ordinary约化与超奇异约化分析，研究整除两个Drinfeld奇异模之差的素理想的分裂行为；利用算法计算给定阶的CM j-不变量多项式，并通过universality论证证明该算法对极大阶计算的是Hilbert模多项式。

Result: 证明了CM且为S-单位的秩2 Drinfeld模j-不变量集合的有限性；给出了计算CM j-不变量的算法；提供了反驳Dorman猜想的显式反例。

Conclusion: 建立了Drinfeld模在函数域情形下与椭圆曲线经典理论类似的有限性结果，同时通过反例修正了领域内的错误猜想，并为计算Hilbert模多项式提供了有效算法。

Abstract: We prove the finiteness of the set of $j$-invariants of Drinfeld modules of rank 2 over $\mathbb{F}_q[T]$ which are CM and $S$-units, for $S$ the infinite set of primes with even degrees. The proof is based on the study of ordinary reduction and supersingular reduction of Drinfeld modules, and on the splitting behaviour of primes dividing the difference of two Drinfeld singular moduli. We also provide an algorithm to compute a polynomial with coefficients in $\mathbb{F}_q[T]$ and roots the $j$-invariants having CM by a given order, and use it to compute some explicit examples, providing for instance counterexamples to a conjecture of Dorman. For a maximal order $\mathcal{O}$, we prove by a universality argument that our algorithm computes the Hilbert modular polynomial $H_\mathcal{O}$.

</details>


### [141] [Recent Progress around Cohen-Lenstra Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06024)
*Jordan S. Ellenberg*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文回顾了Cohen-Lenstra启发式方法的发展历程，包括Wood及其合作者提出的广义Cohen-Lenstra猜想、Smith关于类群2-部分的定理（解决了椭圆曲线极简猜想），以及Koymans和Pagano在ℓ-素数情况下的工作（证明了Stevenhagen关于负Pell方程的猜想）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cohen-Lenstra启发式方法是算术统计中一个核心但几乎完全未证明的基础性问题，需要对随机二次域的类群N-挠部分的分布进行理论解释。

Method: 通过综述方式，梳理了三个主要方向的发展：Wood等的拓扑学支持的广义Cohen-Lenstra猜想、Smith关于Selmer群在二次扭中行为的一般性定理、以及Koymans和Pagano在ℓ-素数情况下的扩展工作。

Result: Smith证明了类群2-素部分的Cohen-Lenstra猜想并解决了椭圆曲线极简猜想；Koymans和Pagano证明了Stevenhagen关于负Pell方程的猜想。

Conclusion: Cohen-Lenstra相关问题近年来取得了快速进展，从完全未证明的状态发展到在特定情形下获得突破性定理，但仍有许多公开问题有待解决。

Abstract: In 1983, Henri Cohen and Hendrik Lenstra proposed a conjecture about the distribution of the N-torsion of the class group of a random quadratic field, supported by what was at the time a large amount of computational evidence. The Cohen-Lenstra heuristics, which are still almost entirely unproven, have become one of the central foundational problems in arithmetic statistics. Recent years have seen a rapidly accelerated pace of development in Cohen-Lenstra problems. I will give a tour of these developments, including the work of Wood and her collaborators developing a fully fleshed out roster of generalized Cohen-Lenstra conjectures, with support from topology; Smith's theorems proving the Cohen--Lenstra conjectures for the 2-primary part of the class group, as part of more general theorems about Selmer groups in quadratic twists, leading to a resolution of the minimalist conjecture for elliptic curves; and recent work by Koymans and Pagano in the ell-primary case, expanding on Smith's work and proving Stevenhagen's conjecture on the negative Pell equation.

</details>


### [142] [An Elementary Analysis of the Prime Partition Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06068)
*Asaf Cohen Antonir,Asaf Shapira*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文用初等方法证明了将n写成素数之和的方式数pp(n)满足对数渐近公式log pp(n) ~ 2π√(n/(3 log n))，该方法简洁且易于推广到相关问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有更精确的估计但证明复杂冗长，本文旨在提供一种简短、初等的证明方法，并能灵活推广到其他相关问题。

Method: 采用初等组合与数论方法，避免了复杂解析工具，给出简洁的渐近推导。

Result: 证明了log pp(n) ~ 2π√(n/(3 log n))，该结果与已知精确结果一致但证明更简短。

Conclusion: 该方法易于推广到其他类似的加性问题，为相关渐近估计提供了统一的初等框架。

Abstract: Let $pp (n)$ denote the number of ways to write $n$ as a sum of primes. In this paper, we show that $$ \log pp (n) \sim 2π\sqrt{\frac{n}{3\log n}}\;. $$ While sharper estimates are already known, they rely on highly involved and lengthy proofs. In sharp contrast, our approach uses a short, elementary recipe that easily adapts to yield similar asymptotic estimates for several related, extensively studied problems.

</details>


### [143] [A Generalization of the Amdeberhan-Andrews-Ballantine Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06086)
*Rong Chen,Tianjian Xu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Amdeberhan、Andrews和Ballantine关于双重Lambert级数猜想的一个推广形式，该推广由Cui、Kumar和Singh的问题所启发，建立了一个系数由广义除数函数σ_k(n)给出的恒等式，并将原始猜想作为特例包含在内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Cui、Kumar和Singh关于该猜想是否存在推广形式的提问所驱动，旨在探索并建立更一般的数学恒等式。

Method: 通过解析数论和级数变换技术，构造并证明了一个包含广义除数函数σ_k(n)作为系数的恒等式。

Result: 成功建立了Amdeberhan-Andrews-Ballantine猜想的一个推广形式，新恒等式的系数由广义除数函数σ_k(n)给出。

Conclusion: 该推广恒等式成立，且原始猜想作为其特例被包含在内，回应了Cui、Kumar和Singh关于推广可能性的问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove a generalization of a conjecture of Amdeberhan, Andrews, and Ballantine on double Lambert series. Motivated by a question raised by Cui, Kumar, and Singh concerning the existence of a generalization of this conjecture, we establish an identity in which the coefficients are given by the generalized divisor function $σ_k(n)$. As a special case, our result includes the original conjecture.

</details>


### [144] [On a conjecture on Romanoff type sumsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06118)
*Yuchen Ding,Liangxun Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文推广了Erdős关于Romanoff型表示函数的k阶矩上界的经典结果，并在Hardy-Littlewood猜想条件下给出了陈永高关于Romanoff型sumsets猜想的条件性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Erdős(1950)关于Romanoff型表示函数k阶矩上界的经典结果，并利用该推广在Hardy-Littlewood猜想条件下证明陈永高关于Romanoff型sumsets的猜想。

Method: 通过推广Erdős 1950年的经典结果，结合Hardy-Littlewood猜想，对Romanoff型表示函数的k阶矩上界进行一般化处理，并应用于证明sumsets相关猜想。

Result: 给出了Romanoff型表示函数k阶矩上界的一般化结果，并在Hardy-Littlewood猜想条件下条件性地证明了陈永高的猜想。

Conclusion: 论文成功推广了Erdős的结果，并为陈永高的猜想提供了条件性证明，展示了Hardy-Littlewood猜想在数论问题中的重要应用。

Abstract: In this note, we generalize a 1950 result of P. Erd\H os on upper bounds of $k$-th moment of Romanoff type representation functions. As an application, we give a conditional proof of a recent conjecture of Y.-G. Chen on Romanoff type sumsets under the assumption of the Hardy-Littlewood conjecture.

</details>


### [145] [No three algebraic conjugates of degree sixteen sum to zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06222)
*Žygimantas Baronėnas,Paulius Drungilas,Jonas Jankauskas*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究最小正整数d（不被3整除），使得存在一个Q上的d次代数数，其三个代数共轭之和为零。通过分类16个顶点上6度顶点传递图，证明d≠16，结合前人结果得出d=20。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定最小的、不被3整除的正整数d，使得存在一个Q上的d次代数数，其三个代数共轭之和为零。该问题与代数数论中代数共轭的线性关系相关。

Method: 采用16个顶点上6度顶点传递图的分类方法，通过图论工具排除d=16的可能性，并结合Dubickas、Smyth、Stong、Jankauskas及Virbalas等人的已有结果推导出d=20。

Result: 证明了d≠16，进而结合已有文献结果得到d=20。

Conclusion: 所求的最小正整数d为20。

Abstract: Let $d$ be the smallest positive integer, not divisible by $3$, for which there exists an algebraic number over $\mathbb{Q}$ of degree $d$ whose some three algebraic conjugates sum to zero. Employing the classification of vertex-transitive graphs on 16 vertices of degree 6, we prove that $d\neq 16$. This, combined with results obtained by Dubickas, Smyth and Stong \cite{DubickasSmyth2006}, Dubickas and Jankauskas \cite{DubickasJankauskas2015} and Virbalas \cite{Virbalas2025a}, implies that $d=20$.

</details>


### [146] [Arithmetic statistics of isogeny Selmer groups associated to hyperelliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06327)
*Martí Oller*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过结合Bhargava的几何数论方法与Vinberg理论（基于B型和C型Dynkin图表示），确定了与亏格g≥2超椭圆曲线雅可比簇相关的某种同源Selmer群平均大小的渐近结果，并利用Greenberg-Wiles公式证明了这些同源Selmer群平均大小的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高亏格超椭圆曲线雅可比簇上同源Selmer群的平均大小，这是数论中关于椭圆曲线Selmer群平均大小研究向高维情况的推广。

Method: 将Bhargava的几何数论方法与Vinberg理论相结合，利用与B型和C型Dynkin图相关的表示构造新的参数化；同时应用Greenberg-Wiles公式导出下界。

Result: 得到了这些同源Selmer群平均大小的渐近结果以及一些下界估计。

Conclusion: 成功将Selmer群平均大小的研究从椭圆曲线情形推广到高亏格超椭圆曲线的雅可比簇，展示了几何数论与Vinberg理论的结合在该问题上的有效性。

Abstract: We determine asymptotic results for the average size of Selmer groups arising from certain isogenies related to Jacobians of hyperelliptic curves of genus $g\geq 2$. We do so by combining Bhargava's geometry-of-numbers methods with new parametrisations coming from Vinberg theory, arising from representations related to the Dynkin diagrams of type $B$ and $C$. We additionally prove some lower bounds on the average size of these isogeny Selmer groups by using a formula of Greenberg--Wiles.

</details>
